Universality of empirical risk minimization
Authors: Andrea Montanari, Basil Saeed
First: 2022-02-17T18:53:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T17:04:48+00:00
Comments: 90 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study a general class of optimization problems with decision variable $\boldsymbolΘ \in \mathbb{R}^{p \times k}$ and cost function which is the sum of $n$ terms, each dependent on $\boldsymbolΘ$ through the $k$-dimensional projection $\boldsymbolΘ^\top \boldsymbol{x}_i$, where $\boldsymbol{x}_i$, $i \leq n$ are i.i.d. random vectors. This setting is general enough to include examples of current interest in statistical physics, high-dimensional statistics, and statistical learning theory. We consider the proportional asymptotics $n, p \to \infty$, with $n/p = Θ(1)$, and prove that, whenever there exists a minimizer satisfying a suitable generalization of a "delocalization" condition, the minimum value is universal. Namely, (for subgaussian $\boldsymbol{x}_i$) it depends on the distribution of $\boldsymbol{x}_i$ only through its asymptotic mean and covariance. This delocalization condition is essentially necessary. Earlier universality results for such problems were limited to strongly convex loss functions. We derive applications of our theory to statistical learning and prove general universality results both for train and (under additional conditions) test error. In particular, we establish universality for vectors $\boldsymbol{x}_i$ generated by random 1-layer neural networks (random features models) and first-order Taylor approximations of 2-layer networks (neural tangent models). Finally, we establish that the delocalization property holds for a class of statistical learning problems under a condition that is easy to verify.
中文标题/摘要
标题：经验风险最小化的普适性
我们研究一类具有决策变量 $\boldsymbolΘ \in \mathbb{R}^{p \times k}$ 且成本函数为 $n$ 项之和的优化问题，每项依赖于 $\boldsymbolΘ$ 通过 $k$ 维投影 $\boldsymbolΘ^\top \boldsymbol{x}_i$，其中 $\boldsymbol{x}_i$（$i \leq n$）为独立同分布随机向量。该设定足够通用，可涵盖统计物理、高维统计与统计学习理论中当前关注的实例。我们考虑比例渐近情形 $n, p \to \infty$，且 $n/p = Θ(1)$，并证明：只要存在满足某种“去局域化”条件推广的最小值点，最小值即为普适的。即（对于子高斯 $\boldsymbol{x}_i$），其仅依赖于 $\boldsymbol{x}_i$ 分布的渐近均值与协方差。该去局域化条件本质上是必要的。此前针对此类问题的普适性结果仅限于强凸损失函数。我们推导了理论在统计学习中的应用，并证明了训练误差（及在附加条件下测试误差）的普适性。特别地，我们建立了随机单层神经网络（随机特征模型）及二层网络的一阶泰勒近似（神经切线模型）生成的向量 $\boldsymbol{x}_i$ 的普适性。最后，我们证明在易于验证的条件下，一类统计学习问题满足去局域化性质。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand generalization behavior in high-dimensional statistical learning, this paper investigates empirical risk minimization under a broad class of optimization problems where the cost function depends on projections of data vectors through a parameter matrix. The authors analyze the asymptotic behavior as sample size and dimension grow proportionally, proving that under a delocalization condition on minimizers, the minimum value becomes universal—depending only on the mean and covariance of the input data distribution. This result extends prior universality findings, which were restricted to strongly convex losses, and applies to both training and test error in learning models, including random feature and neural tangent kernels. The delocalization condition is shown to be necessary and verifiable in practice.
为理解高维统计学习中的泛化行为，本文研究了一类广泛优化问题，其决策变量与成本函数依赖于独立同分布随机向量的投影。在样本量n与维度p按比例增长且n/p为常数的渐近条件下，作者证明：只要最小值点满足某种“去局域化”条件，经验风险的最小值即为普适的，仅依赖于输入分布的渐近均值与协方差（对子高斯输入成立），该条件被证明是必要的，且扩展了此前仅适用于强凸损失函数的普适性结果。理论应用于随机特征模型与神经切线模型，证明训练误差与测试误差的普适性，并指出该去局域化条件在许多实际学习场景中易于验证。
Morphing into Hybrid Attention Models
Authors: Disen Lan, Jianbin Zheng, Yuxi Ren, Xin Xia, Xuanda Wang, Xuefeng Xiao, Xipeng Qiu, Yu Cheng
First: 2026-06-29T17:02:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T17:02:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hybrid attention models improve long-context efficiency by retaining only a subset of full-attention layers and replacing the remaining layers with linear attention. However, the effectiveness of Transformer-to-hybrid conversion critically depends on which layers preserve full attention. Existing hybrid layer selection methods typically rely on heuristic strategies such as fixed placement patterns or layerwise scoring, implicitly treating layer importance as isolated and overlooking the interdependent layer effect under a global hybrid configuration. In this work, we formulate hybrid layer selection as a budget-constrained subset optimization problem. We further propose FlashMorph (Fast LAyer Selection for Hybrid MORPHing), an effective, efficient and scalable layer selection method for Transformer-to-hybrid conversion. FlashMorph first constructs a morphable model by equipping each full-attention layer with a converted linear-attention branch. It then freezes all model weights and jointly optimizes layerwise gates on synthetic long-context retrieval data, with a linearization regularization that encourages the model to rely on linear attention for efficiency. The learned gates are discretized under a preset full-attention budget to instantiate the hybrid architecture, followed by standard logits distillation and long-context finetuning. Extensive experiments show that FlashMorph discovers more effective hybrid configurations, preserves strong long-context recall and general benchmark performance while substantially reducing layer selection cost compared with existing layer selection methods, demonstrating its effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：形态转换为混合注意力模型
混合注意力模型通过仅保留部分全注意力层，并将剩余层替换为线性注意力，以提升长上下文效率。然而，Transformer转混合模型的有效性高度依赖于哪些层保留全注意力。现有混合层选择方法通常依赖启发式策略，如固定位置模式或逐层评分，隐式地将层重要性视为孤立，忽视了全局混合配置下层间的相互依赖效应。本文将混合层选择形式化为带预算约束的子集优化问题，并提出FlashMorph（用于混合形态转换的快速层选择），一种高效、有效且可扩展的Transformer转混合模型层选择方法。FlashMorph首先通过为每个全注意力层配备转换后的线性注意力分支构建可形态转换模型；随后冻结所有模型权重，联合在合成长上下文检索数据上优化层级门控，辅以线性化正则化以鼓励模型依赖线性注意力提升效率。学习到的门控在预设的全注意力预算下离散化，以实例化混合架构，再进行标准logits蒸馏与长上下文微调。大量实验表明，FlashMorph发现更有效的混合配置，在保持强长上下文召回与基准性能的同时，显著降低层选择成本，验证其有效性、效率与可扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently handle long contexts in Transformers, this work addresses the challenge of selecting which layers retain full attention in hybrid attention models. It proposes FlashMorph, a method that formulates layer selection as a budget-constrained optimization problem and uses a morphable model with frozen weights to jointly optimize layerwise gates on synthetic data under linearization regularization. Experimental results show FlashMorph achieves superior hybrid configurations with strong long-context recall and benchmark performance while significantly reducing selection cost compared to heuristic methods.
为提升Transformer在长上下文处理中的效率，本文提出FlashMorph方法，将混合注意力层选择建模为带预算约束的子集优化问题。该方法为每个全注意力层配备线性注意力分支，冻结权重后在合成长上下文数据上联合优化层门控，辅以线性化正则化以鼓励模型依赖线性注意力。学习到的门控经离散化后生成混合架构，并通过标准蒸馏与长上下文微调。实验表明，FlashMorph在保持长上下文召回率和基准性能的同时，显著降低层选择成本，优于现有启发式方法。
When AI Benchmarks Plateau: A Systematic Study of Benchmark Saturation
Authors: Mubashara Akhtar, Anka Reuel, Prajna Soni, Sanchit Ahuja, Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi, Ruchit Rawal, Vilém Zouhar, Srishti Yadav, Chenxi Whitehouse, Dayeon Ki, Jennifer Mickel, Leshem Choshen, Marek Šuppa, Jan Batzner, Jenny Chim, Jeba Sania, Yanan Long, Hossein A. Rahmani, Christina Knight, Yiyang Nan, Jyoutir Raj, Yu Fan, Shubham Singh, Subramanyam Sahoo, Eliya Habba, Usman Gohar, Siddhesh Pawar, Robert Scholz, Arjun Subramonian, Jingwei Ni, Mykel Kochenderfer, Sanmi Koyejo, Mrinmaya Sachan, Stella Biderman, Zeerak Talat, Avijit Ghosh, Irene Solaiman
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-18T16:51:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T17:01:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence benchmarks are an important mechanism for measuring model progress and guiding deployment decisions. However, benchmarks quickly "saturate", making it difficult to differentiate models and diminishing their long-term value. In this study, we define benchmark saturation and analyze it across 60 language model benchmarks using 14 properties that relate to saturation. We find that nearly half of the our benchmarks exhibit saturation, with rates increasing with age. Further, we find that resilience to saturation is impacted by expert-curation, not by public test data. Our results suggest that design choices can extend benchmark longevity and inform more durable evaluation approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当AI基准测试达到瓶颈：一项基准饱和的系统性研究
人工智能基准测试是衡量模型进展和指导部署决策的重要机制。然而，基准测试会迅速“饱和”，导致难以区分模型并削弱其长期价值。本研究定义了基准饱和，并通过14个与饱和相关的属性，对60个语言模型基准测试进行了分析。我们发现近一半的基准测试存在饱和现象，且饱和率随基准测试年龄增长而上升。此外，我们发现抗饱和能力受专家筛选影响，而非公共测试数据影响。研究结果表明，设计选择可延长基准测试寿命，并指导更持久的评估方法。

The Human Creativity Benchmark
Authors: Aspen Hopkins, Allison Nulty, Alexandria Minetti, Anoop Pakki, Angad Singh
First: 2026-06-29T16:59:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:59:46+00:00
Comments: 30 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern AI evaluation frameworks treat evaluator disagreement as noise to be resolved. In creative domains, professional disagreement reflects genuine differences in taste, not measurement error. We argue that evaluating creative AI requires preserving two distinct signals: convergence, where professionals align around shared best practices, and divergence, where individual taste legitimately varies. We present the Human Creativity Benchmark (HCB), a benchmark that operationalizes this separation by collecting pairwise preferences, scalar ratings on prompt adherence, usability, and visual appeal, and qualitative rationale from domain professionals. Across 15,000 professional judgments spanning five creative domains and three workflow phases (ideation, mockup, refinement), we find that convergence concentrates on verifiable dimensions like technical correctness and visual hierarchy, while divergence concentrates on taste-driven dimensions like aesthetic direction and conceptual risk. No model excels uniformly across all phases. Collapsing these signals into a single quality metric discards the most actionable information: where models must be correct versus where they should remain steerable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人类创造力基准
现代AI评估框架将评估者分歧视为需消除的噪声。但在创意领域，专业评估者的分歧反映的是真实品味差异，而非测量误差。我们认为，评估创意AI需保留两种不同信号：收敛性（专业人士围绕共同最佳实践达成一致）与发散性（个体品味的合理差异）。我们提出了人类创造力基准（HCB），通过收集成对偏好、对提示遵循度、可用性及视觉吸引力的评分，以及领域专家的定性理由，实现这一分离。在涵盖五个创意领域和三个工作阶段（构思、草图、精修）的15,000项专业判断中，我们发现收敛性集中于可验证维度（如技术正确性与视觉层次），而发散性集中于品味驱动维度（如美学方向与概念风险）。无模型在所有阶段均表现卓越。将这些信号合并为单一质量指标，会丢失最具操作价值的信息：模型在哪些方面必须正确、哪些方面应保持可控。
Summary / 总结
The paper argues that in creative AI evaluation, professional disagreement should not be treated as noise but as meaningful signals reflecting taste-based divergence and shared convergence. To capture this, the Human Creativity Benchmark (HCB) collects pairwise preferences, scalar ratings on prompt adherence, usability, and visual appeal, plus qualitative rationales from domain experts across five creative domains and three workflow phases. Results show convergence primarily reflects objective criteria like technical correctness and visual hierarchy, while divergence reflects subjective aesthetic and conceptual choices. No model performs uniformly well across all phases, and collapsing signals into a single metric loses critical insights about where models must be accurate versus where they should remain steerable.
该论文认为，在创意AI评估中，专业评审意见的分歧不应被视为噪声，而应视为反映审美偏好差异与共识的有意义信号。为此，作者提出了“人类创意基准”（HCB），通过收集领域专家的成对偏好、对提示遵循度、可用性与视觉吸引力的评分及定性理由，覆盖15,000个判断，涉及五个创意领域和三个工作阶段。结果表明，共识集中在可验证维度（如技术正确性与视觉层次），而分歧则体现主观审美与概念风险。无模型在所有阶段表现一致，若将这些信号合并为单一质量指标，将丢失关键信息——即模型在哪些环节需准确、哪些环节需保持可控性。
The Neglected Baseline in Model Interpretation
Authors: Yongjin Cui, Xiaohui Fan
First: 2026-05-21T12:40:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:59:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We observe that existing model interpretation methods generally ignore the baseline, and such neglect often results in imprecise or even incorrect interpretation. In this paper, we reformulate the task of model interpretation and the interpretation principles for model interpretation results to demonstrate the importance of the baseline. For the first time, we unify gradient-based methods, Integrated Gradients (IG), and Taylor expansion, clarify the relationships among the three, and explicitly identify the corresponding baseline for each method. This may have a significant impact on the further performance improvement of some gradient-based schemes. On this basis, we analyze the flaws and errors in related model interpretation methods (IG, LayerCAM, ODAM, Difference Map). We advocate evaluating the quality of model interpretation results precisely through the attribution error between the attribution result and the attribution target, rather than adopting flawed evaluation methods, such as those based on marginal-effect or the assumption of perfect model performance. We revise IG and develope a model interpretation method with a clear and reasonable baseline, achieving better results. Our method supports model interpretation based on features from any layer. Interpretation based on features from different layers are all reasonable, and the differences among these results reflect varying degrees of feature extraction at different feature extraction stages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型解释中被忽视的基线
我们观察到现有模型解释方法普遍忽略基线，这种忽视常导致解释不精确甚至错误。本文重新定义模型解释任务及解释结果的原则，以凸显基线的重要性。首次统一梯度法、集成梯度（IG）与泰勒展开，阐明三者关系并明确各自对应的基线。这可能对部分梯度法的进一步性能提升产生重大影响。在此基础上，我们分析相关模型解释方法（IG、LayerCAM、ODAM、Difference Map）的缺陷与错误，主张通过归因结果与归因目标间的归因误差精确评估解释质量，而非采用基于边际效应或完美模型性能假设等错误评估方法。我们修订IG并开发一种具有清晰合理基线的模型解释方法，取得更好效果。该方法支持基于任意层特征的模型解释，不同层特征的解释均合理，其差异反映不同特征提取阶段的特征提取程度差异。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the overlooked role of baselines in model interpretation, showing that ignoring them leads to imprecise or incorrect attributions. It reformulates interpretation principles and unifies gradient-based methods, Integrated Gradients, and Taylor expansion, explicitly assigning baselines to each. The authors analyze flaws in existing methods like IG, LayerCAM, ODAM, and Difference Map, arguing that attribution error—measured against the true attribution target—is a superior evaluation metric. They revise Integrated Gradients to use a clear baseline, improving interpretability and enabling layer-agnostic feature-based explanations, where results from different layers reflect varying levels of abstraction.
本文指出当前模型解释方法常忽略基线，导致解释结果不准确。作者重构了模型解释任务与原则，统一了梯度法、集成梯度与泰勒展开，为每种方法明确对应基线。他们批判了IG、LayerCAM、ODAM和Difference Map等方法因基线错误和评估指标不当而产生的缺陷，主张用归因误差衡量解释质量。所提出的改进IG方法具备清晰基线，提升解释效果，并支持任意层特征的解释，不同层结果差异反映不同抽象层级的特征提取程度。
TraceLab: Characterizing Coding Agent Workloads for LLM Serving
Authors: Kan Zhu, Mathew Jacob, Chenxi Ma, Yi Pan, Stephanie Wang, Arvind Krishnamurthy, Baris Kasikci
First: 2026-06-29T16:59:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:59:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Coding agents are rapidly becoming a major application of agentic LLMs, but serving them efficiently remains challenging. Progress on this challenge requires understanding real workload patterns, yet the data needed for such analysis is largely absent. Existing public traces and benchmarks do not capture real, day-to-day coding-agent usage across multiple agents and model families for serving-system analysis. To help fill this gap, we collect and release a trace of roughly 4,300 coding-agent sessions, containing about 350,000 LLM steps and 430,000 tool calls from our own day-to-day use of Claude Code and Codex. Our analysis shows that coding-agent workloads feature long autonomous loops, long contexts with short outputs, diverse and heavily-tailed tool calls, and high but imperfect prefix cache hit rates. These findings point to concrete opportunities for optimizing serving, including lower-overhead tool calling, append-length-aware prefill, semantic-aware tool-latency prediction, and improved KV-cache management around human-paced gaps. We release the dataset, trace collection pipeline, and analysis code at https://github.com/uw-syfi/TraceLab.git; the project website is https://tracelab.cs.washington.edu.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TraceLab：刻画编码代理在大语言模型服务中的工作负载
编码代理正迅速成为自主型大语言模型的重要应用场景，但高效服务它们仍具挑战。解决此问题需理解真实工作负载模式，然而相关数据稀缺。现有公开追踪数据与基准测试未能覆盖多代理、多模型家族在实际日常编码代理使用中的服务系统分析需求。为填补这一空白，我们收集并发布约4300个编码代理会话的追踪数据，包含约35万次大语言模型步骤和43万次工具调用，源自我们日常使用Claude Code与Codex的实践。分析表明，编码代理工作负载具有长自主循环、长上下文短输出、工具调用多样且分布偏斜、高但不完美的前缀缓存命中率等特点。这些发现为优化服务提供了具体方向，包括降低开销的工具调用、感知追加长度的预填充、语义感知的工具延迟预测及优化人类节奏间隙下的KV缓存管理。我们已将数据集、追踪采集流程及分析代码开源于https://github.com/uw-syfi/TraceLab.git；项目网站为https://tracelab.cs.washington.edu。

Motivated by the need to understand real-world coding-agent workloads for efficient LLM serving, the authors collected and analyzed a trace of 4,300 coding-agent sessions involving 350,000 LLM steps and 430,000 tool calls from daily use of Claude Code and Codex. Their analysis reveals that these workloads exhibit long autonomous loops, long contexts with short outputs, diverse and heavily-tailed tool calls, and high but imperfect prefix cache hit rates. These insights suggest concrete optimization opportunities such as reducing tool-call overhead, implementing append-length-aware prefill, predicting tool latency semantically, and improving KV-cache management around human-paced pauses.

Convergence of Continual Learning in Homogeneous Deep Networks
Authors: Matan Schliserman, Gon Buzaglo, Itay Evron, Daniel Soudry
First: 2026-06-29T16:55:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:55:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We characterize weakly regularized continual classification in homogeneous models as sequential projections onto task margin sets. This result generalizes prior analyses restricted to either stationary (single-task) deep models or continual linear models. We show that global convergence generally fails, even for simple models linear in data but nonlinear in parameters. Nevertheless, by leveraging results from nonconvex projection theory, we identify regularity properties of homogeneous deep networks that guarantee local linear convergence under random and cyclic task sequences. Finally, we extend our analysis to continual regression, unifying the framework for homogeneous models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：同质深度网络中持续学习的收敛性
我们将弱正则化的同质模型中持续分类问题表征为对任务边距集的序列投影。该结果推广了此前仅限于静态（单任务）深度模型或持续线性模型的分析。我们证明，即使对于数据线性但参数非线性的简单模型，全局收敛通常也失败。然而，通过利用非凸投影理论的结果，我们识别出同质深度网络的正则性条件，可保证在随机和循环任务序列下实现局部线性收敛。最后，我们将分析扩展至持续回归，统一了同质模型的框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand convergence behavior in continual learning with deep networks, this work characterizes weakly regularized continual classification as sequential projections onto task margin sets, generalizing prior analyses limited to stationary or linear models. It demonstrates that global convergence typically fails even for simple linear-in-data but nonlinear-in-parameters models, yet identifies sufficient regularity conditions in homogeneous deep networks that ensure local linear convergence under random or cyclic task sequences, leveraging nonconvex projection theory. The framework is further extended to continual regression, unifying analysis across homogeneous models.
为理解深度网络在持续学习中的收敛行为，本文将弱正则化持续分类建模为对任务边距集的序列投影，推广了此前仅针对静态或线性模型的分析。研究发现，即使对于简单线性于数据但非线性于参数的模型，全局收敛通常也失败。然而，通过应用非凸投影理论，作者识别出同质深度网络中确保在随机或循环任务序列下局部线性收敛的特定正则性条件。该框架进一步扩展至持续回归，统一了同质模型的分析。
Agent libOS: A Runtime Substrate for Capability-Controlled Self-Evolving LLM Agents
Authors: Yingqi Zhang
First: 2026-06-02T16:53:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:54:16+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 1 figure, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents are becoming long-running software actors rather than fixed tool users. They accumulate memory, activate skills, synthesize tools, fork children, attach remote resources, and commit checkpoints into reusable execution images. These mechanisms improve adaptability, but also create a systems-security failure mode: if exposing an action also grants the authority needed to perform it, self-evolution becomes a permission-escalation path. This paper presents Agent libOS, an agent-native library-OS substrate for capability-controlled self-evolving agents. Its central invariant is that model-visible affordances may evolve while resource authority changes only through explicit, audited runtime primitives. Agent libOS represents an agent as an AgentProcess with process identity, process-local Object Memory, message queues, a tool table, loaded Skills, process-local Deno/TypeScript JIT tools, child processes, budgets, checkpoints, and explicit capabilities. AgentImage objects define boot-time prompt and tool-table state; Skills and JIT tools extend the action surface; checkpoint-derived images make internal state reusable. None of these mechanisms grants filesystem, shell, human, memory, process, checkpoint, image, JSON-RPC, MCP, or PTY authority by itself. The prototype implements process-local namespaces, persistent runtime state, LLM-call observability, human approval queues, budgets, syscall-mediated JIT tools, trusted Runtime Modules, Object-bound PTY sessions, checkpoint restore/fork/commit, JSON-RPC and MCP providers, and a deterministic runtime-safety benchmark. On 27 versioned deterministic tasks, it completed the task plans while preventing all modeled unauthorized side effects, with a 7.0% conservative false-denial rate. Simple wrapper and sandbox baselines preserved task completion but failed most safety checks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Agent libOS：一种面向能力控制自演化大语言模型代理的运行时基座
大语言模型（LLM）代理正从固定工具使用者演变为长期运行的软件实体。它们积累记忆、激活技能、合成工具、派生子进程、挂载远程资源，并提交检查点以生成可复用的执行镜像。这些机制提升了适应性，但也引入了系统安全风险：若暴露某项操作的同时也赋予执行该操作所需的权限，自演化便可能成为权限提升的路径。 本文提出Agent libOS，一种面向能力控制自演化代理的原生库操作系统基座。其核心不变式为：模型可见的操作能力可演化，而资源权限仅通过显式、可审计的运行时原语改变。Agent libOS将代理表示为一个AgentProcess，包含进程身份、进程本地对象内存、消息队列、工具表、已加载技能、进程本地Deno/TypeScript JIT工具、子进程、预算、检查点及显式能力。AgentImage对象定义启动时提示与工具表状态；技能与JIT工具扩展操作面；由检查点派生的镜像使内部状态可复用。上述机制均不单独赋予文件系统、Shell、人类用户、内存、进程、检查点、镜像、JSON-RPC、MCP或PTY权限。 原型系统实现了进程本地命名空间、持久化运行时状态、LLM调用可观测性、人类审批队列、预算、系统调用介导的JIT工具、可信运行时模块、对象绑定的PTY会话、检查点恢复/派生/提交、JSON-RPC与MCP提供者，以及确定性运行时安全基准。在27个版本化确定性任务中，系统成功完成任务计划，同时阻止所有建模的未授权副作用，保守性误拒率为7.0%。简单封装与沙箱基线虽能完成任务，但多数安全检查失败。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security risks of self-evolving LLM agents that can escalate permissions through exposed actions, this paper introduces Agent libOS, a runtime substrate that enforces capability control. It models agents as AgentProcesses with explicit capabilities, local memory, tool tables, and checkpointable states, ensuring that resource authority changes only via audited primitives. Experimental results on 27 deterministic tasks show the prototype successfully completed all task plans while preventing unauthorized side effects, with a 7.0% conservative false-denial rate, outperforming simple sandbox baselines that failed most safety checks.
为了解决自演化LLM代理通过暴露动作导致权限提升的安全风险，本文提出了Agent libOS，一种支持能力控制的库操作系统。它将代理建模为具备明确权限、本地内存、工具表和可检查点状态的AgentProcess，确保资源权限仅通过审计过的运行时原语变更。在27个确定性任务的实验中，原型系统成功完成所有任务计划，同时阻止所有未授权副作用，保守误拒率为7.0%，优于仅能完成任务但多数安全检查失败的简单沙箱基线。
Internalized Reasoning for Long-Context Visual Document Understanding
Authors: Austin Veselka
First: 2026-03-31T04:41:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:52:45+00:00
Comments: 9 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual long-document understanding is critical for enterprise, legal, and scientific applications, yet the best performing open recipes have not explored reasoning, a capability which has driven leaps in math and code performance. We introduce a synthetic data pipeline for reasoning in long-document understanding that generates thinking traces by scoring each page for question relevance, extracting textual evidence and ordering it from most to least relevant. We apply SFT to the resulting traces within \texttt{<think>} tags, gated by a \texttt{<cot>} control token, and the resulting reasoning capability is internalized via low-strength model merging. We study Qwen3 VL 32B and Mistral Small 3.1 24B. With Qwen3 VL, we achieve 58.3 on MMLongBenchDoc, surpassing the 7$\times$ larger Qwen3 VL 235B A22B (57.0). With Mistral, we show that synthetic reasoning outperforms distillation from the Thinking version's traces by 3.8 points on MMLBD-C, and internalized reasoning exhibits 12.4$\times$ fewer mean output tokens compared to explicit reasoning. We release our pipeline for reproducibility and further exploration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：长上下文视觉文档理解的内化推理
