Summary / 总结
To enhance visual long-document understanding for enterprise and scientific applications, this work introduces a synthetic data pipeline that generates reasoning traces by scoring page relevance, extracting textual evidence, and ordering it by relevance. These traces are used to fine-tune models via supervised fine-tuning with a control token, and reasoning is internalized through low-strength model merging. Experiments on Qwen3 VL 32B and Mistral Small 3.1 24B show that internalized reasoning achieves 58.3 on MMLongBenchDoc, outperforming the larger 235B variant by 1.3 points, and reduces output token count by 12.4x compared to explicit reasoning while beating distillation by 3.8 points on MMLBD-C.
为提升长上下文视觉文档理解中的推理能力，该研究提出一种合成数据生成管道，通过评估页面相关性、提取文本证据并按相关性排序生成推理轨迹，再结合监督微调与低强度模型融合实现推理能力内化。在Qwen3 VL 32B和Mistral Small 3.1 24B模型上实验表明，该方法在MMLongBenchDoc上得分58.3，超越更大规模的235B模型；在MMLBD-C上比蒸馏方法高3.8分，且平均输出词数减少12.4倍。
EcoVideo: Entropy-Orchestrated Video Generation Paradigm in Cloud-Edge Dynamics
Authors: Jiayu Chen, Hengyi Zhang, Maoliang Li, Minyu Li, Zihao Zheng, Xuanzhe Liu, Guojie Luo, Xiang Chen
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-29T16:51:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:51:56+00:00
Comments: EcoVideo is honored to be accepted by ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
DiT video generation is latency-intensive due to iterative full-frame denoising, while prior cloud-edge methods largely rely on static inter-step decoupling and cannot leverage inter-frame similarity or adapt to system dynamics. We propose EcoVideo, an entropy-orchestrated framework for dynamic inter-frame decoupling: early-stage self-attention entropy provides a training-free estimate of frame-wise information density for frame selection; a cloud large model denoises sparse high-entropy keyframes; and an edge lightweight model reconstructs the remaining frames via motion-aware interpolation with refinement for temporal stability. EcoVideo further adapts the keyframe budget and edge refinement depth to real-time bandwidth and compute availability, optimizing end-to-end latency under constraints. Experiments on representative DiT video generators show improved quality--efficiency trade-offs and up to 2.9x end-to-end speedup in low-bandwidth, compute-limited edge settings. Code is available at https://github.com/IF-LAB-PKU/EcoVideo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EcoVideo：面向云边动态的熵引导视频生成范式
DiT视频生成因迭代全帧去噪而延迟高，此前云边方法多依赖静态跨步解耦，无法利用帧间相似性或适应系统动态。我们提出EcoVideo，一种熵引导的动态帧间解耦框架：早期自注意力熵提供无训练的帧级信息密度估计以选择关键帧；云端大模型对稀疏高熵关键帧去噪；边缘轻量模型通过运动感知插值与精细化重建剩余帧，确保时序稳定性。EcoVideo进一步根据实时带宽与计算资源动态调整关键帧预算与边缘精修深度，在约束下优化端到端延迟。在代表性DiT视频生成器实验中，EcoVideo实现更优的质量-效率权衡，并在低带宽、计算受限边缘场景下实现最高2.9倍端到端加速。代码见https://github.com/IF-LAB-PKU/EcoVideo。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high latency of DiT video generation due to iterative full-frame denoising and the inability of prior cloud-edge methods to adapt to system dynamics, EcoVideo introduces an entropy-orchestrated framework for dynamic inter-frame decoupling. It uses early-stage self-attention entropy to estimate frame-wise information density for selective frame processing, offloading high-entropy keyframes to a cloud large model for denoising, and delegating remaining frames to an edge lightweight model that reconstructs them via motion-aware interpolation with refinement. The system dynamically adjusts keyframe budget and edge refinement depth based on real-time bandwidth and compute availability, optimizing end-to-end latency. Experiments demonstrate improved quality-efficiency trade-offs and up to 2.9x speedup in low-bandwidth, compute-limited edge environments.
为解决DiT视频生成因迭代全帧去噪导致的高延迟，以及传统云边方法依赖静态帧解耦无法利用帧间相似性或适应系统动态的问题，EcoVideo提出一种基于熵引导的动态帧解耦框架。该框架利用早期自注意力熵无训练地估计帧级信息密度以选择关键帧，将高熵关键帧交由云端大模型去噪，其余帧则在边缘设备上通过运动感知插值与精修实现时序稳定。系统根据实时带宽与计算资源动态调整关键帧预算与边缘精修深度，优化端到端延迟。实验表明，在低带宽、计算受限的边缘环境下，该方法在质量与效率之间取得更优平衡，最高可实现2.9倍的端到端加速。
Poller: Are LLMs Suitable for Evaluating the Poetry Understanding Task?
Authors: Shanshan Wang, Derek F. Wong, Jingming Yao, Lidia S. Chao
First: 2026-06-29T16:51:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:51:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional automatic evaluation methods have been shown to be unsuitable for modern Chinese poetry because of the distinct nature of this literary genre. Human evaluation remains reliable, but is expensive and not applicable to large-scale data. In this paper, we propose Poller (Poetry LLM Evaluator), a novel method leveraging large language models (LLMs) to evaluate the poetry understanding task. Specifically, our method requires LLMs to play the role of a poem's author with detailed information, thereby emulating human evaluation and judgment by adopting the poet's perspective. We conducted comprehensive experiments on multiple LLMs, evaluating the interpretations of poems across eight specialized dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that our method effectively reduces the evaluation error between LLMs and humans. Especially for specific dimension evaluation, Poller-based LLMs achieve a 94.55% and 89.53% error reduction for rhetorical techniques and defamiliarization, respectively, compared to baseline methods. These performances are unattainable by conventional LLM evaluation methods. Experimental results from multiple LLMs across various dimensions validate the efficacy of our method. This work bridges the gap between automated efficiency and human expertise, establishing a foundation for automated evaluation in poetry-related tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Poller：大语言模型适合评估诗歌理解任务吗？
传统自动评估方法因现代中国诗歌的独特文学属性而不适用。人工评估虽可靠，但成本高且难以扩展至大规模数据。本文提出Poller（诗歌大语言模型评估器），利用大语言模型（LLM）评估诗歌理解任务。具体而言，该方法要求LLM扮演诗人角色，结合详尽信息，从诗人视角模拟人类评估与判断。我们在多个LLM上进行综合实验，从八个专业维度评估诗歌解读。实验表明，该方法显著降低LLM与人类评估间的误差。尤其在特定维度评估中，基于Poller的LLM在修辞技巧和陌生化维度分别实现94.55%和89.53%的误差降低，远超基线方法。多LLM跨维度实验验证了该方法的有效性。本研究弥合了自动化效率与人类专业评估之间的鸿沟，为诗歌相关任务的自动化评估奠定基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional automatic evaluation methods for modern Chinese poetry and the impracticality of human evaluation at scale, this paper introduces Poller, a method that uses large language models to evaluate poetry understanding by having them assume the role of poets. The approach leverages the LLMs' ability to interpret poems from the author’s perspective, mimicking human judgment across eight specialized dimensions. Experiments across multiple LLMs show that Poller significantly reduces evaluation error compared to baseline methods, achieving 94.55% and 89.53% error reduction in rhetorical techniques and defamiliarization respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness and superiority over conventional automated evaluation approaches.
鉴于传统自动评估方法难以应对现代中文诗歌的独特性，且人工评估成本高、难以规模化，作者提出Poller方法，通过让大语言模型扮演诗人角色，从作者视角评估诗歌理解结果。实验在八个专业维度上验证，该方法相较基线方法显著降低评估误差，其中修辞技巧和陌生化维度分别降低94.55%和89.53%，展现出对人类评价的高度契合，为诗歌理解任务的自动化评估提供了可行框架。
Linguistic Firewall: Geometry as Defense in Multi-Agent Systems Routing
Authors: Dvir Alsheich, Adar Peleg, Ben Hagag, Rom Himelstein, Amit Levi, Avi Mendelson
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-29T16:51:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:51:06+00:00
Comments: 8 pages (9 more for appendix), 3 figures. Published at the Second Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond (AIWILD) at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) has driven the evolution of Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), where specialized agents collaborate to execute complex workflows. Effective orchestration in these environments requires robust routing mechanisms to efficiently allocate tasks to the most suitable agent. However, existing routers fundamentally rely on unverified proxies, ranging from textual self-descriptions to static surrogate representations, to gauge an agent's competence. This reliance on non-empirical data creates a critical gap between an agent's projected profile and its actual operational capabilities, introducing severe security vulnerabilities. Malicious agents can easily misrepresent their proficiencies or harbor covert backdoors that evade both standard external analysis and static representation-learning techniques. In this work, we introduce ANTAP (Automatic Non-Textual Agent Picker), an evaluation-driven routing architecture that discards indirect proxies in favor of active capability testing. By dynamically querying agents to ascertain their true competencies empirically, ANTAP distills performance into fixed behavioral operators within a shared semantic space. At inference time, routing is performed via a purely non-textual algebraic projection, establishing a "linguistic firewall" that renders metadata-based attacks inexpressible. In our experiments, ANTAP achieves near-zero ASR against description-based injection attacks, compared to 67.3\% and above for the description-based router baseline. Against adaptive embedding attacks, ANTAP achieves substantially lower ASR than the embedding-based baseline, with a 20\% reduction, while remaining resilient to description manipulation by design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言防火墙：多智能体系统路由中的几何防御
大型语言模型（LLMs）的快速集成推动了多智能体系统（MAS）的发展，其中专业智能体协作执行复杂工作流。此类环境中的有效编排需要稳健的路由机制，以高效地将任务分配给最合适的智能体。然而，现有路由器依赖未经验证的代理（从文本自描述到静态代理表示）来评估智能体能力，这种对非经验数据的依赖导致智能体预期画像与其实际操作能力之间存在关键差距，从而引入严重安全漏洞。恶意智能体可轻易伪装自身能力或隐藏隐蔽后门，逃避标准外部分析和静态表示学习技术。本文提出ANTAP（自动非文本智能体选择器），一种基于评估的路由架构，摒弃间接代理，转而采用主动能力测试。通过动态查询智能体以实证确定其真实能力，ANTAP将性能提炼为共享语义空间中的固定行为算子。推理时，路由通过纯非文本代数投影完成，建立“语言防火墙”，使基于元数据的攻击无法表达。实验表明，与基于描述的路由基线相比，ANTAP对描述注入攻击的误接受率接近零（基线为67.3%以上）；对自适应嵌入攻击，ANTAP误接受率显著低于嵌入基线（降低20%），且设计上对描述操纵具有鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security risks posed by unverified textual proxies in multi-agent systems routing, this work introduces ANTAP, an evaluation-driven architecture that replaces proxy-based agent profiling with active empirical testing. ANTAP dynamically queries agents to measure true competencies, encoding results into fixed behavioral operators within a shared semantic space, enabling non-textual algebraic routing that acts as a linguistic firewall. Experimental results show ANTAP achieves near-zero attack success rate against description-based injection attacks, outperforming baseline routers by 67.3%+ reduction, and reduces attack success rate by 20% against adaptive embedding attacks while being inherently resistant to description manipulation.
为应对多智能体系统路由中依赖未经验证文本代理所引发的安全风险，本文提出ANTAP架构，通过主动能力测试替代传统代理评估。ANTAP动态查询智能体以提取真实能力，将其性能编码为共享语义空间中的固定行为算子，实现纯非文本代数路由，构建语言防火墙。实验表明，ANTAP对基于描述的注入攻击攻击成功率接近零，较基线提升67.3个百分点；对自适应嵌入攻击成功率降低20%，且天然抵御描述篡改。
Energy-Efficient Plant Monitoring via Knowledge Distillation
Authors: Ilyass Moummad, Reda Bensaid, Kawtar Zaher, Hervé Goëau, Jean-Christophe Lombardo, Joseph Salmon, Pierre Bonnet, Alexis Joly
First: 2026-04-29T20:27:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:50:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in large-scale visual representation learning have significantly improved performance in plant species and plant disease recognition tasks. However, state-of-the-art models, often based on high-capacity vision transformers or multimodal foundation models, remain computationally expensive and difficult to deploy in resource-constrained environments such as mobile or edge devices. This limitation hinders the scalability of automated biodiversity monitoring and precision agriculture systems, where efficiency is as critical as accuracy. In this work, we investigate knowledge distillation as an effective approach to transfer the representational capacity of large pretrained models into smaller, more efficient architectures. We focus on plant species and disease recognition, and conduct an extensive empirical study on two challenging benchmarks: Pl@ntNet300K-v2 and Deep-Plant-Disease. We evaluate four representative architectures, including two ConvNeXt models and two vision transformers, under multiple training regimes: from-scratch training and pretrained initialization, each with and without distillation. In total, we train and evaluate 70 models. Our results show that knowledge distillation consistently improves performance across tasks and architectures. Distilled models are able to match the performance of significantly larger models while maintaining substantially lower computational cost. These findings demonstrate the potential of knowledge distillation techniques to enable efficient and scalable deployment of plant recognition systems in real-world environmental applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过知识蒸馏实现节能的植物监测
大规模视觉表征学习的最新进展显著提升了植物物种和植物病害识别任务的性能。然而，当前最先进的模型，常基于高容量视觉Transformer或多模态基础模型，仍计算成本高昂，难以部署在移动或边缘设备等资源受限环境中。这一局限性阻碍了自动化生物多样性监测和精准农业系统的可扩展性，其中效率与准确性同等重要。本文研究知识蒸馏作为将大型预训练模型的表征能力迁移到更小、更高效架构的有效方法。我们聚焦于植物物种和病害识别，并在两个具有挑战性的基准数据集Pl@ntNet300K-v2和Deep-Plant-Disease上进行广泛实证研究。我们评估了四种代表性架构（包括两个ConvNeXt模型和两个视觉Transformer），在多种训练策略下：从头训练和预训练初始化，每种策略均包含和不包含蒸馏。总计训练和评估70个模型。结果表明，知识蒸馏在各类任务和架构中均能持续提升性能。蒸馏模型能够在保持显著更低计算成本的同时，匹配大幅更大的模型性能。这些发现展示了知识蒸馏技术在现实环境应用中实现高效、可扩展植物识别系统部署的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient plant recognition systems deployable on edge devices, this study explores knowledge distillation to compress large vision models into smaller, computationally lighter architectures without sacrificing accuracy. The method transfers knowledge from pretrained large models—such as ConvNeXt and vision transformers—to compact learners via distillation, evaluated across two plant recognition benchmarks. Experimental results from 70 trained models show that distilled architectures consistently match or exceed the performance of their larger counterparts while consuming significantly less computational resources, demonstrating the viability of distillation for scalable, energy-efficient biodiversity and agricultural monitoring.
为满足资源受限环境下植物识别的高效需求，本研究探索知识蒸馏技术，将大型视觉模型压缩为更小、更易部署的架构，同时保持精度。研究人员在两个植物识别基准上评估了四种模型（两种ConvNeXt和两种视觉Transformer），分别从零训练和使用预训练权重，并在有无蒸馏条件下进行对比。共训练评估70个模型，结果表明蒸馏始终提升性能，使蒸馏模型在大幅降低计算开销的同时匹配大型模型的精度，从而支持在边缘和移动设备上的可扩展部署。
How to Train Your Long-Context Visual Document Model
Authors: Austin Veselka
First: 2026-02-16T23:26:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:49:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present the first comprehensive, large-scale study of training long-context vision language models up to 344K context, targeting long-document visual question answering with measured transfer to long-context text. While several such strong are open-weight, namely Qwen3 VL and GLM 4.5/6V, their training recipes and data pipelines are not reproducible. We systematically study continued pretraining, supervised finetuning, and preference optimization for 24B and 32B parameter models, backed by extensive LC evaluations and ablations to bridge this gap, and achieve state-of-the-art performance on MMLongBenchDoc for both parameter scales. In addition to this, our key findings include: (i) training on context lengths that match evaluation context lengths outperforms training on longer contexts, (ii) training and evaluating with page indices provides a simple, high-impact boost to long-document performance, (iii) our synthetic data pipelines enable self-improvement via continued pretraining and supervised finetuning, and (iv) we extend the known text-to-visual long context transfer to the reverse, showing that visual long context training transfers to long-context text performance. We also release MMLBD-C, a manually corrected version of MMLongBenchDoc to reduce erroneous and low quality examples in the benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何训练长上下文视觉文档模型
我们首次系统性地研究了训练最大上下文长度达344K的长上下文视觉语言模型，目标是长文档视觉问答，并通过长上下文文本的迁移评估。尽管已有多个开源权重模型（如Qwen3 VL和GLM 4.5/6V）表现强劲，但其训练方案和数据流程不可复现。我们系统性研究了24B和32B参数模型的持续预训练、监督微调与偏好优化，辅以大量LC评估和消融实验，弥合这一差距，并在MMLongBenchDoc上实现了两个参数规模的最先进性能。此外，我们的主要发现包括：(i) 训练上下文长度与评估上下文长度匹配时表现优于更长上下文；(ii) 使用页索引进行训练和评估可显著提升长文档性能；(iii) 我们的合成数据流程支持通过持续预训练和监督微调实现自我提升；(iv) 我们将已知的文本到视觉长上下文迁移扩展至反向方向，表明视觉长上下文训练可迁移至长上下文文本性能。我们还发布了MMLBD-C，即MMLongBenchDoc的人工修正版本，以减少基准数据中的错误和低质量样本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reproducible training methods for long-context visual language models, this study systematically evaluates continued pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and preference optimization for 24B and 32B parameter models, achieving state-of-the-art results on MMLongBenchDoc. Key findings include that matching training and evaluation context lengths yields better performance, page indices during training significantly improve long-document results, synthetic data pipelines enable iterative self-improvement, and visual long-context training transfers to long-context text tasks. The authors also release a corrected version of the benchmark to enhance data quality.
为解决长上下文视觉语言模型训练方法不可复现的问题，本研究系统评估了24B和32B参数模型的持续预训练、监督微调与偏好优化，取得MMLongBenchDoc上的最先进性能。主要发现包括：训练上下文长度与评估长度匹配效果更优、页码索引显著提升长文档表现、合成数据管道支持持续自我改进、视觉长上下文训练可迁移到文本长上下文任务。作者同时发布了修正版基准数据以提升数据质量。
Training Vision-Language-Action Models with Dense Embodied Chain-of-Thought Supervision
Authors: Haoyang Li, Guanlin Li, Youhe Feng, Chen Zhao, Zhuoran Wang, Yang Li, Qizhe Wei, Shifeng Bao, Haitao Shen, Yihan Zhao, Tong Yang, Jing Zhang
First: 2026-06-29T16:48:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:48:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Cross-embodiment transfer in vision-language-action (VLA) models remains challenging because low-level state and action spaces differ fundamentally across robot platforms. We observe that the high-level cognitive process underlying manipulation, including scene perception, object identification, task planning, and sub-task decomposition, is largely shared across embodiments. Based on this observation, we present ZR-0, a 2.6 billion parameter end-to-end VLA model that uses dense Embodied Chain-of-Thought (ECoT) supervision to align cross-embodiment representations within the vision-language model (VLM). ZR-0 adopts a dual-stream architecture: a pre-trained VLM (System 2) generates structured ECoT reasoning during training, while a Diffusion Transformer-based action expert (System 1) produces continuous action chunks via flow matching. The two components are coupled through cross-attention, with an attention mask that restricts the action expert to input prompt features only, enabling ECoT generation to be entirely skipped at inference without any performance loss. ZR-0 is pre-trained on ProcCorpus-60M, a large-scale dataset comprising approximately 60 million frames (approximately 1,000 hours) from over 400K trajectories, with dense ECoT annotations covering 96.8% of all frames. We evaluate ZR-0 on three simulation benchmarks spanning single-arm (LIBERO), bimanual (RoboTwin 2.0), and humanoid (RoboCasa GR-1 Tabletop) embodiments, as well as real-world experiments on the xArm platform, demonstrating strong performance across all settings. Code and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/RUCKBReasoning/ZR-0.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用密集具身思维链监督训练视觉-语言-动作模型
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的跨具身迁移仍具挑战性，因为不同机器人平台的低层状态和动作空间存在根本差异。我们观察到，操控背后的高层认知过程——包括场景感知、物体识别、任务规划和子任务分解——在不同具身间基本共享。基于此观察，我们提出ZR-0，一个26亿参数的端到端VLA模型，利用密集具身思维链（ECoT）监督，在视觉-语言模型（VLM）内对齐跨具身表示。ZR-0采用双流架构：预训练的VLM（系统2）在训练中生成结构化ECoT推理，而基于扩散Transformer的动作专家（系统1）通过流匹配生成连续动作片段。两个组件通过交叉注意力耦合，注意力掩码限制动作专家仅输入提示特征，从而在推理时可完全跳过ECoT生成而不损失性能。ZR-0在ProcCorpus-60M数据集上预训练，该数据集包含约6000万帧（约1000小时）来自40万条轨迹的视频，密集ECoT标注覆盖所有帧的96.8%。我们在三个模拟基准（单臂LIBERO、双臂RoboTwin 2.0、人形RoboCasa GR-1桌面）及真实xArm平台实验中评估ZR-0，表现优异。代码与模型检查点见https://github.com/RUCKBReasoning/ZR-0。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of cross-embodiment transfer in vision-language-action models due to differing low-level state and action spaces, the authors propose ZR-0, a 2.6 billion parameter model that leverages dense Embodied Chain-of-Thought (ECoT) supervision to align high-level cognitive processes across robotic platforms. The model employs a dual-stream architecture: a pre-trained vision-language model generates structured ECoT reasoning during training, while a Diffusion Transformer-based action expert produces continuous actions via flow matching, connected through cross-attention with an attention mask that restricts action generation to prompt features. This design allows ECoT reasoning to be skipped at inference without performance loss. Pre-trained on ProcCorpus-60M — a dataset of 60 million frames from 400K trajectories with 96.8% ECoT annotation coverage — ZR-0 achieves strong performance across simulation benchmarks (LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, RoboCasa GR-1 Tabletop) and real-world xArm experiments, demonstrating robust cross-embodiment generalization.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型在不同机器人平台间跨具身迁移的挑战（因低层状态与动作空间差异显著），作者提出ZR-0模型，利用密集的具身思维链（ECoT）监督对齐不同平台间的高层认知过程。该模型采用双流架构：预训练视觉-语言模型在训练中生成结构化ECoT推理，而基于扩散Transformer的动作专家通过流匹配生成连续动作，二者通过带注意力掩码的交叉注意力耦合，掩码确保动作专家仅接收提示特征，从而推理阶段可完全跳过ECoT生成而不损失性能。模型在ProcCorpus-60M数据集上预训练，该数据包含约6000万帧（约1000小时）来自40万轨迹的视频，覆盖96.8%的帧具有密集ECoT标注。实验在LIBERO（单臂）、RoboTwin 2.0（双臂）、RoboCasa GR-1 Tabletop（人形）仿真基准及真实xArm平台测试中均表现优异，验证了其跨具身泛化能力。
Bridging the NISQ and Fault-Tolerant Regimes: Generative-ML-Assisted Quantum Selected CI for Molecular Simulations
Authors: Anurag K. S. V., Ashish Kumar Patra, Manas Mukherjee, Ruchika Bhat, Sai Shankar P., Rahul Maitra, Jaiganesh G
First: 2026-06-29T16:48:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:48:44+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Calculation of binding energies for protein-ligand molecular systems requires accurate treatment of the electronic structure, a quantum chemistry problem that scales exponentially on classical hardware, while current quantum hardware remains too noisy for the required circuit depths. This report presents a hybrid quantum-classical workflow performed on the Fujitsu FX700 ideal state-vector simulator using QARP that addresses two structural inefficiencies in quantum-sampling-based diagonalization workflows. First, we integrate the Linear Scaling CNOT UCCSD (LCNot-UCCSD) ansatz into the QSCI framework, replacing the $\mathcal{O}(N^6)$ CCSD parameter initialization of the competing LUCJ ansatz approach with $\mathcal{O}(N^4)$ MP2-amplitude initialization. Second, we introduce QSCI-RBM, a variant that replaces the configuration recovery of the SQD framework with a Restricted Boltzmann Machine (RBM) acting as a compact generative subspace expansion model. Both are evaluated on eight different molecules in STO-3G across 14 controlled artificial error levels with 100 independent runs each, validated on potential energy surface scans of the N$_2$ molecule in cc-pVDZ, and embedded within DMET to treat the FDA-approved antiviral Amantadine (C$_{10}$H$_{17}$N, 11 DMET fragments) and the active region of the SARS-CoV-2 main protease complexed with its covalent inhibitor Carmofur (PDB: 7BUY, C$_{15}$H$_{28}$N$_4$O$_5$S, 10 fragments). To our knowledge, this is the first deployment of LCNot-UCCSD within QSCI on a quantum computing simulator, and the first DMET-QSCI(LCNot-UCCSD)-RBM application to an industry-relevant protein-ligand system. By utilizing a fraction of the classical computing resources required by the current state-of-the-art work by Cleveland Clinic, RIKEN, and IBM Quantum, this approach enables more efficient and economical drug discovery simulations for the industry.
中文标题/摘要
标题：连接NISQ与容错时代：基于生成机器学习的量子选择态CI方法用于分子模拟
蛋白质-配体分子体系结合能的计算需要精确处理电子结构，这是一个在经典硬件上呈指数级增长的量子化学问题，而当前量子硬件噪声仍过高，难以满足所需电路深度。本报告在富士通FX700理想态向量模拟器上使用QARP，提出了一种混合量子-经典工作流程，解决了量子采样基对角化工作流中的两个结构效率问题。首先，我们将线性缩放CNOT UCCSD（LCNot-UCCSD）变分形式集成到QSCI框架中，用O(N⁴)的MP2振幅初始化替代竞争方法LUCJ的O(N⁶) CCSD参数初始化。其次，我们引入QSCI-RBM，用受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）替代SQD框架的构型恢复，作为紧凑的生成子空间扩展模型。两种方法均在STO-3G基组下对八种不同分子进行评估，每种在14种受控人工误差水平下独立运行100次，验证结果基于N₂分子在cc-pVDZ基组下的势能面扫描，并嵌入DMET框架处理FDA批准的抗病毒药物金刚烷胺（C₁₀H₁₇N，11个DMET片段）及SARS-CoV-2主蛋白酶与共价抑制剂卡莫氟（PDB: 7BUY，C₁₅H₂₈N₄O₅S，10个片段）的活性区域。据我们所知，这是首次在量子计算模拟器上将LCNot-UCCSD应用于QSCI框架，也是首次将DMET-QSCI(LCNot-UCCSD)-RBM应用于工业相关的蛋白-配体体系。相比克利夫兰医学中心、理化学研究所和IBM量子团队当前最先进的方法，该方法仅需其所需经典计算资源的一小部分，从而为行业提供更高效、更经济的药物发现模拟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to compute accurate binding energies for protein-ligand systems without being limited by classical scaling or current quantum noise, this work proposes a hybrid quantum-classical method using the Fujitsu FX700 simulator and QARP framework. It introduces LCNot-UCCSD, an ansatz with O(N⁴) MP2 initialization replacing the O(N⁶) CCSD initialization of prior approaches, and QSCI-RBM, which replaces configuration recovery with a Restricted Boltzmann Machine for compact subspace expansion. Evaluated on eight molecules across 14 error levels and validated on N₂ and two complex drug systems (Amantadine and Carmofur), the method achieves comparable accuracy to prior state-of-the-art while using significantly less classical computing resources, enabling more efficient molecular simulations for drug discovery.
为解决蛋白质-配体体系结合能计算中经典计算指数级增长与当前量子硬件噪声过大的矛盾，该研究提出一种混合量子-经典方法，基于富士通FX700模拟器和QARP框架。其核心是引入LCNot-UCCSD，将CCSD参数初始化成本从O(N^6)降至O(N^4)，并采用QSCI-RBM替代配置恢复，用受限玻尔兹曼机实现紧凑的子空间扩展。该方法在八种分子上测试14种人工误差水平，验证了N2势能面扫描结果，并成功应用于DMET框架下的Amantadine和SARS-CoV-2主蛋白酶-卡莫氟复合物等工业相关体系。相比Cleveland Clinic、RIKEN和IBM Quantum的最新工作，该方法在保持精度的同时大幅降低经典计算资源需求，为药物发现提供更高效、经济的模拟方案。
To Tab or Not to Tab: Measuring Critical Engagement in AI Code Completion Tools Using Behavioral Signals and Attention Checks
Authors: Jessica Hutchison, Ian Tyler Applebaum, Kenneth Angelikas, Kush Rakesh Patel, Phuoc Nguyen, Antonio Lazaro, Nicholas Rucinski, Rahad Arman Nabid, Stephen MacNeil
Venue: Proceedings of the 31st ACM Conference on Innovation and Technology in Computer Science Education (ITiCSE 2026), Madrid, Spain, July 10-15, 2026
First: 2026-06-29T16:47:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:47:46+00:00
Comments: 7 pages. Accepted for publication in the Proceedings of the 31st ACM Conference on Innovation and Technology in Computer Science Education (ITiCSE 2026), Madrid, Spain, July 10-15, 2026. Author's accepted manuscript
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI code completion tools, such as Github Copilot, provide students with code suggestions to help them write programs. However, recent qualitative studies suggest that students fail to critically evaluate these suggestions. We present Clover, a code completion tool that logs students' interactions with code suggestions and additionally offers attention checks to probe reflective engagement during programming tasks. We also develop a taxonomy of behavioral interaction metrics for AI-assisted programming, informed by literature. We analyzed relationships between interaction patterns, engagement with attention checks, and task performance. We observed that higher rates of tab accept were associated with lower attention check performance, while increased dwell time was associated with higher attention check performance. We conclude by discussing how programming process data and attention checks might support reflective engagement in AI-assisted programming.
中文标题/摘要
标题：该使用Tab键还是不使用？——利用行为信号与注意力检查衡量AI代码补全工具中的批判性参与
AI代码补全工具（如GitHub Copilot）为学生提供代码建议以帮助其编写程序。然而，近期定性研究表明，学生未能批判性评估这些建议。我们提出了Clover，一款记录学生与代码建议交互行为并提供注意力检查的代码补全工具，以探查编程任务中的反思性参与。我们还基于文献开发了AI辅助编程行为交互指标的分类体系。我们分析了交互模式、注意力检查参与度与任务表现之间的关系。观察到，Tab键接受率越高，注意力检查表现越差；而停留时间越长，注意力检查表现越好。最后，我们讨论了编程过程数据与注意力检查如何支持AI辅助编程中的反思性参与。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by evidence that students often fail to critically evaluate AI-generated code suggestions, this study introduces Clover, a tool that logs user interactions and administers attention checks to measure reflective engagement. Using a taxonomy of behavioral metrics derived from existing literature, the researchers analyzed how interaction patterns—such as tab acceptance rate and dwell time—correlate with attention check performance and task outcomes. Results showed that frequent tab acceptance was linked to poorer attention check performance, while longer dwell times correlated with better attention check scores, suggesting that passive acceptance may signal shallow engagement.
鉴于学生可能未能批判性评估AI代码建议，本研究提出Clover工具，记录用户交互行为并辅以注意力检查以衡量反思性参与。基于文献构建的行为指标分类体系，研究分析了交互模式（如Tab接受率与停留时长）与注意力检查表现及任务完成度的关系。结果表明，频繁接受Tab建议与较低注意力检查得分相关，而较长的停留时间则与更高的注意力检查表现相关，暗示被动接受建议可能反映浅层参与。
Self-Supervised Learning of Plant Image Representations
Authors: Ilyass Moummad, Kawtar Zaher, Hervé Goëau, Jean-Christophe Lombardo, Pierre Bonnet, Alexis Joly
First: 2026-04-30T07:41:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:46:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated plant recognition plays a crucial role in biodiversity monitoring and conservation, yet current approaches rely heavily on supervised learning, which is limited by the availability of expert-labeled data. Self-supervised learning (SSL) offers a scalable alternative, but existing methods and training protocols are largely designed for coarse-grained visual tasks and may not transfer well to fine-grained domains such as plant species recognition. In this work, we investigate SSL for plant image representation learning. We show that commonly used augmentations in SSL pipelines - such as Gaussian blur, grayscale conversion, and solarization - are detrimental in the context of plant images, as they remove subtle discriminative cues essential for fine-grained recognition. We instead identify alternative transformations, including affine and posterization, that are better suited to this domain. We further demonstrate that training SimDINOv2 on the iNaturalist 2021 Plantae subset yields significantly stronger representations than training on ImageNet-1K, highlighting the importance of domain-specific data for SSL. Our findings are consistent across both ViT-Base and ViT-Large architectures. Moreover, our models achieve competitive performance and sometimes outperform strong supervised baselines Pl@ntCLEF and BioCLIP on downstream plant recognition tasks in few-shot settings. Overall, our results highlight the critical importance of domain-adapted augmentation strategies and dataset selection in self-supervised learning, and provide practical guidelines for building scalable models for biodiversity monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：植物图像表示的自监督学习
自动化植物识别在生物多样性监测与保护中至关重要，但当前方法主要依赖监督学习，受限于专家标注数据的可用性。自监督学习（SSL）提供了可扩展的替代方案，但现有方法和训练协议多针对粗粒度视觉任务，难以迁移到植物物种识别等细粒度领域。本文研究植物图像表示的SSL方法。我们发现，SSL流程中常用的增强方法（如高斯模糊、灰度转换和日光化）在植物图像中会破坏细粒度识别所需的细微判别线索。我们识别出更适合该领域的替代变换，包括仿射变换和海报化。进一步表明，以iNaturalist 2021 Plantae子集训练SimDINOv2，其表示能力显著优于ImageNet-1K训练结果，凸显领域特定数据对SSL的重要性。结果在ViT-Base和ViT-Large架构中均一致。此外，我们的模型在少样本植物识别任务中表现优异，有时优于强监督基线Pl@ntCLEF和BioCLIP。整体而言，结果强调了自监督学习中领域适配增强策略和数据集选择的关键性，并为构建可扩展的生物多样性监测模型提供实用指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of labeled plant data and the limitations of existing self-supervised learning methods for fine-grained recognition, this study investigates domain-adapted SSL strategies for plant image representation learning. The authors find that standard SSL augmentations like Gaussian blur and solarization degrade performance by obscuring subtle visual cues critical for species differentiation, and instead propose affine and posterization transformations. Training SimDINOv2 on the iNaturalist 2021 Plantae subset yields stronger representations than ImageNet-1K, and the models achieve competitive or superior performance compared to supervised baselines like Pl@ntCLEF and BioCLIP in few-shot plant recognition tasks, across both ViT-Base and ViT-Large architectures.
鉴于植物图像识别领域标注数据稀缺且现有自监督学习方法难以有效处理细粒度分类任务，本研究探索了针对植物图像的自监督学习策略。实验发现，常用自监督增强方法（如高斯模糊和日光化）会削弱植物物种识别所需的细微视觉特征，因此提出仿射变换和海报化等更适合该领域的增强方式。在iNaturalist 2021 Plantae数据集上训练的SimDINOv2模型表现优于ImageNet-1K，且在少样本植物识别任务中，其性能可与Pl@ntCLEF和BioCLIP等监督基线模型相媲美，甚至超越后者，适用于ViT-Base和ViT-Large两种架构。
StereoGS: Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting via Stereo Priors
Authors: Wenhao Yuan, Yiyuan Ge, Deli Cai
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-29T16:45:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:45:57+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 6 figures, accepted to ECCV 2026, project page: https://stringerywh00.github.io/StereoGS_project_page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has achieved remarkable success in real-time novel view synthesis, yet it suffers from severe overfitting under sparse-view settings due to insufficient geometric constraints. While recent methods introduce monocular depth priors to mitigate this, they inherently struggle with scale ambiguity and cross-view inconsistency, leading to defective geometry. In this paper, we propose StereoGS, a novel sparse-view 3DGS framework that integrates stereo priors to establish reliable binocular consistency. Unlike scale-agnostic monocular constraints, StereoGS introduces a Stereo Depth Regularization by constructing virtual stereo pairs during optimization and leveraging a foundation stereo model to enforce absolute scale and binocular-consistent structures. To further suppress overfitting and eliminate redundant primitives, we design a Gradient-Aware Opacity Decay strategy that dynamically penalizes Gaussians based on their relative opacity gradient magnitudes. Combined with a Consistency-Aware Dense Initialization using zero-shot multi-view depth estimation, StereoGS effectively anchors primitives to accurate scene surfaces. Extensive experiments on LLFF, DTU, Mip-NeRF360, and Blender datasets demonstrate that StereoGS achieves state-of-the-art performance in sparse-view settings without incurring any additional inference overhead. Project Page: https://stringerywh00.github.io/StereoGS_project_page/
中文标题/摘要
标题：StereoGS：基于立体视觉先验的稀疏视角3D高斯点渲染
3D高斯点渲染（3DGS）在实时新视角合成中取得了显著成功，但在稀疏视角设置下因几何约束不足而严重过拟合。尽管近期方法引入单目深度先验缓解此问题，但其固有地面临尺度模糊与跨视角不一致，导致几何缺陷。本文提出StereoGS，一种新颖的稀疏视角3DGS框架，通过整合立体视觉先验建立可靠的双目一致性。不同于无尺度感知的单目约束，StereoGS引入立体深度正则化，在优化过程中构建虚拟立体对，并利用基础立体模型强制执行绝对尺度与双目一致结构。为进一步抑制过拟合并消除冗余基元，我们设计梯度感知不透明度衰减策略，根据基元相对不透明度梯度幅值动态惩罚高斯点。结合零样本多视角深度估计的自适应密集初始化，StereoGS有效将基元锚定在准确的场景表面。在LLFF、DTU、Mip-NeRF360和Blender数据集上的大量实验表明，StereoGS在稀疏视角设置下达到最先进性能，且无额外推理开销。项目页面：https://stringerywh00.github.io/StereoGS_project_page/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the overfitting issue in sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting due to insufficient geometric constraints, StereoGS introduces stereo priors to enforce binocular consistency and absolute scale. It constructs virtual stereo pairs during optimization and uses a foundation stereo model to guide depth regularization, while a gradient-aware opacity decay strategy suppresses overfitting by penalizing redundant Gaussians. Combined with zero-shot multi-view depth initialization, StereoGS anchors primitives accurately to scene surfaces. Experiments on LLFF, DTU, Mip-NeRF360, and Blender show it achieves state-of-the-art sparse-view performance without extra inference cost.
为解决稀疏视角下3D高斯点云因几何约束不足导致的过拟合问题，StereoGS引入立体视觉先验，通过构建虚拟立体对并结合基础立体模型，强制实现视差一致性与绝对尺度约束。同时设计梯度感知的不透明度衰减策略，根据高斯点云相对不透明度梯度动态惩罚冗余点，配合零样本多视角深度初始化，精准锚定场景表面。在LLFF、DTU、Mip-NeRF360和Blender数据集上的实验表明，该方法在稀疏视角下达到最优性能，且无额外推理开销。
Latent Actions from Factorized Transition Effects under Agent Ambiguity
Authors: Heejeong Nam, Chandradithya S Jonnalagadda, Harshit Aggarwal, Eric Xu, Randall Balestriero
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-29T16:45:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:45:04+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026 Workshop on Compositional Learning. Project Page: https://hazel-heejeong-nam.github.io/LAM/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Latent Action Models (LAMs) learn action-like proxies from observation transitions. However, in multi-object or distractor-rich scenes, these visual effects mix agent motion with distractors, camera dynamics, and background changes, making the underlying action source ambiguous without supervision. Structuring this mixture as reusable transition effects provides an intermediate representation from which action-like latents can be more robustly formed. We introduce Observed Transition Factorization (OTF), which decomposes each transition into a sparse set of observed transition primitives. Using these primitives as the transition interface, we propose OTF-LAM, which abstracts motion primitives into action-like latents within the standard inverse-forward dynamics framework, and OTF-LAM-Dino, a decoder-free variant that predicts future states in a frozen DINOv2 representation space. Empirically, OTF primitives transfer zeroshot across controlled carrier and morphology shifts, showing reusability. Furthermore, downstream policy learning results match or outperform baselines under complex transition ambiguity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在代理模糊性下的因式分解过渡效应的潜在动作
潜在动作模型（LAMs）从观察过渡中学习类似动作的代理。然而，在多物体或干扰丰富的场景中，这些视觉效应混合了代理运动、干扰物、相机动态和背景变化，导致在无监督情况下动作来源模糊。将这种混合结构化为可复用的过渡效应，提供了一种中间表示，从而更稳健地形成类似动作的潜在变量。我们引入了观察过渡分解（OTF），将每个过渡分解为一组稀疏的观察过渡原语。利用这些原语作为过渡接口，我们提出了OTF-LAM，它在标准逆向-前向动力学框架内将运动原语抽象为类似动作的潜在变量；并提出了无解码器变体OTF-LAM-Dino，其在冻结的DINOv2表示空间中预测未来状态。实验证明，OTF原语可在受控载体和形态变化下实现零样本迁移，显示其可复用性。此外，在复杂过渡模糊性下，下游策略学习结果与基线持平或优于基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of disentangling agent actions from ambiguous visual transitions in complex scenes, this work proposes Observed Transition Factorization (OTF) to decompose transitions into sparse, reusable primitives. Using these primitives as an interface, OTF-LAM and its decoder-free variant OTF-LAM-Dino learn action-like latents within inverse-forward dynamics frameworks. Experiments show these primitives transfer zero-shot across object morphology and carrier changes, and downstream policy learning achieves performance matching or exceeding baselines under high transition ambiguity.
为了解决在复杂场景中难以区分代理动作与干扰因素（如相机运动、背景变化）的问题，该研究提出观察过渡因子化（OTF）方法，将视觉过渡分解为稀疏且可复用的过渡基元。这些基元作为过渡接口，用于在逆向-前向动力学框架中构建更鲁棒的动作类潜变量，形成OTF-LAM及其无解码器变体OTF-LAM-Dino。实验表明，OTF基元可在不同载体和形态变化下实现零样本迁移，下游策略学习性能与基线相当或更优，尤其在高过渡模糊环境下表现突出。
Stochastic-Dimension Frozen Sampled Neural Network for High-Dimensional Gross-Pitaevskii Equations on Unbounded Domains
Authors: Zhangyong Liang, Huanhuan Gao
First: 2026-04-10T14:31:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:44:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper introduces the Stochastic-Dimension Frozen Sampled Neural Network (SD-FSNN), a novel computational framework for solving high-dimensional Gross-Pitaevskii equation (GPE) on unbounded domain. The proposed method circumvents the curse-of-dimensionality that plagues traditional discretizations and the computational bottlenecks of gradient-based neural network solvers through a synergistic combination of techniques. First, a prescribed Gaussian envelope encodes the far-field decay of the wavefunction, enabling a space-time separation where the spatial approximation is handled by a frozen, single-hidden-layer neural network with data-driven sampled features. This yields a gradient-free formalism where spatial derivatives are analytically precomputed and time-dependence is evolved via reduced ODEs. Second, a stochastic-dimension sampler provides a conditionally unbiased estimate of the spatial operator by evaluating only a small subset of spatial dimensions at each time step, essentially reducing computational and memory costs. Discrete conservation laws are also enforced, ensuring long-term stability. Extensive numerical experiments on GPE in up to 1000 dimensions demonstrate that SD-FSNN achieves significantly higher accuracy and efficiency compared to state-of-the-art methods, including PINNs, randomized feature methods, and tensor-network approaches. The results confirm that SD-FSNN effectively mitigates the Kolmogorov $n$-width barrier for frozen-basis models on structured solution manifolds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于无界域高维 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程的随机维度冻结采样神经网络
本文提出了一种新颖的计算框架——随机维度冻结采样神经网络（SD-FSNN），用于求解无界域上的高维 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程（GPE）。该方法通过多种技术的协同作用，规避了传统离散化方法所面临的维度灾难以及基于梯度的神经网络求解器的计算瓶颈。首先，通过预设的高斯包络编码波函数的远场衰减特性，实现时空分离：空间逼近由一个冻结的、单隐藏层神经网络处理，其特征由数据驱动采样获得；由此构建无梯度形式，其中空间导数预先解析计算，时间演化通过降维常微分方程实现。其次，随机维度采样器在每一步仅评估空间维度的子集，从而提供条件无偏的空间算子估计，显著降低计算与内存开销。同时，离散守恒律被强制执行，确保长期稳定性。在高达 1000 维的 GPE 数值实验中，SD-FSNN 显著优于现有方法（包括物理信息神经网络 PINNs、随机特征方法及张量网络方法），在精度与效率上均表现优异。结果证实，SD-FSNN 有效克服了冻结基模型在结构化解流形上所面临的 Kolmogorov $n$-宽度障碍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational intractability of high-dimensional Gross-Pitaevskii equations on unbounded domains, this work proposes the Stochastic-Dimension Frozen Sampled Neural Network (SD-FSNN), which combines a Gaussian envelope to model far-field decay with a frozen, single-hidden-layer neural network for spatial approximation. The method avoids gradient-based optimization by precomputing spatial derivatives and evolving time via reduced ODEs, while a stochastic-dimension sampler reduces computational cost by evaluating only a subset of spatial dimensions at each step. Experimental results across up to 1000 dimensions show SD-FSNN outperforms PINNs, randomized feature methods, and tensor-network approaches in both accuracy and efficiency, demonstrating its ability to overcome the Kolmogorov n-width barrier for structured solutions.
为解决高维无界域上Gross-Pitaevskii方程的计算难题，本文提出随机维度冻结采样神经网络（SD-FSNN），结合高斯包络建模波函数衰减与冻结单隐层神经网络进行空间近似。该方法通过解析预计算空间导数并以降维ODE演化时间依赖性，避免梯度优化。随机维度采样器在每步仅评估部分空间维度，显著降低计算与内存开销，同时保持无偏估计。实验表明，在高达1000维场景下，SD-FSNN在精度与效率上优于PINNs、随机特征法及张量网络方法，有效突破冻结基模型在结构化解流形上的Kolmogorov n-宽度瓶颈。
TRACE: Temporal Relationship-Aware Conversational Entrainment Detection in Dyadic Speech
Authors: Sathvik Manikantan Napa Ugandhar, Hao Zhang, Alison Gunzler, Yuzhe Wang, Thomas Thebaud, Georgi Tinchev, Venkatesh Ravichandran, Laureano Moro-Velázquez
First: 2026-06-29T16:42:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:42:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the proliferation of speech AI agents, understanding emotional entrainment in conversational interaction has become increasingly important. Emotional entrainment is shaped by social relationships and conversational context, influencing affective coordination over time. We introduce DyadEE, a dataset for emotional entrainment detection in dyadic speech interactions, containing both emotionally entrained conversations and synthetic interactions where entrainment is disrupted through partner swapping and emotion resynthesis. We further propose TRACE, a window-level framework that models dyadic interaction as ordered sequences of acoustic embeddings derived from emotion fine-tuned Whisper representations, treating each sample as an interaction trace rather than pooled utterances. Experimental results on DyadEE show that incorporating conversational context and relationship information improves emotional entrainment detection, with TRACE achieving the best accuracy of 97.01%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRACE：面向双人对话的时序关系感知情感同步检测
随着语音AI代理的普及，理解对话互动中的情感同步变得日益重要。情感同步受社会关系和对话语境影响，影响情感在时间上的协调。我们引入DyadEE数据集，用于双人语音互动中的情感同步检测，包含真实情感同步对话及通过伙伴替换和情绪重合成的合成对话（情感同步被破坏）。我们进一步提出TRACE框架，该框架在窗口级别建模双人互动为有序的声学嵌入序列（基于情绪微调的Whisper表示），将每个样本视为交互轨迹而非聚合语句。在DyadEE上的实验表明，结合对话语境和关系信息可提升情感同步检测效果，TRACE达到97.01%的最佳准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand emotional entrainment in human-agent conversational interactions, the authors introduce DyadEE, a novel dataset featuring both naturally entrained and artificially disrupted dyadic speech exchanges. They propose TRACE, a window-level framework that models conversations as sequences of emotion-fine-tuned acoustic embeddings derived from Whisper, treating each interaction as a temporal trace rather than pooled utterances. Experiments on DyadEE demonstrate that incorporating conversational context and relationship dynamics significantly improves detection accuracy, with TRACE achieving 97.01%—the highest performance among evaluated methods.
随着语音AI代理的普及，准确检测对话中的情绪同步变得至关重要，因其受社会关系和对话上下文影响。作者构建了DyadEE数据集，包含自然情绪同步对话与通过伙伴替换和情绪重合成的干扰对话，用于评估。他们提出TRACE框架，将对话建模为基于情绪微调的声学嵌入序列，将每次互动视为时间轨迹而非聚合语句。实验表明，结合对话上下文和关系信息显著提升检测效果，TRACE达到97.01%的准确率，为当前最优方法。
Most Current Model Organisms Are Leaky: Perplexity Differencing Often Reveals Finetuning Objectives
Authors: Mohammed Abu Baker, Luca Baroni, Dan Wilhelm
First: 2026-05-01T18:00:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:38:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Finetuning can significantly modify the behavior of large language models, including introducing harmful or unsafe behaviors. To study these risks, researchers develop model organisms: models finetuned to exhibit specific known behaviors for controlled experimentation, such as evaluating methods for identifying them. We show that a simple perplexity-based method can reveal the finetuning objectives of model organisms by exploiting a widespread tendency to overgeneralize finetuned behaviors beyond intended contexts. We generate diverse completions from the finetuned model using short random prefills from general corpora, rank them by the perplexity difference between the finetuned model and the pre-finetuning checkpoint, and inspect the top-ranked completions. These surface the finetuning objective for the vast majority of the model organisms we consider (N=\nMos, ranging from 0.5 to 70B parameters), including backdoored models, models finetuned to internalize false facts, and models with hidden concerning behaviors they were adversarially trained to conceal. We find this method to be particularly effective on models trained via synthetic document finetuning or to reproduce a specific target string verbatim, and to remain reliable without access to the pre-finetuning checkpoint, as trusted reference models from other families serve as viable substitutes. Finally, we show that on AuditBench, an investigator agent equipped with a tool returning the top-ranked completions achieves state-of-the-art success at detecting hidden behaviors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当前多数模型生物存在漏洞：困惑度差异常可揭示微调目标
微调可显著改变大语言模型的行为，包括引入有害或不安全行为。为研究这些风险，研究人员开发了模型生物：经过微调以展示特定已知行为、便于控制实验的模型，例如评估识别这些行为的方法。我们表明，一种基于困惑度的简单方法可通过利用微调行为普遍超出预期上下文的过度泛化倾向，揭示模型生物的微调目标。我们使用通用语料库中的短随机前缀从微调模型生成多样化的补全，按微调模型与微调前检查点的困惑度差异排序，并检查排名靠前的补全。这些结果揭示了我们所研究的绝大多数模型生物（N=\nMos，参数量从0.5到70B）的微调目标，包括后门模型、内化虚假事实的模型，以及通过对抗训练隐藏其令人担忧行为的模型。我们发现该方法在通过合成文档微调或旨在逐字复现特定目标字符串的模型上尤为有效，且在无微调前检查点的情况下仍保持可靠，因为其他家族的可信参考模型可作为替代。最后，我们在AuditBench上表明，配备返回排名靠前补全工具的调查代理可实现检测隐藏行为的最先进效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect hidden or harmful behaviors introduced during model finetuning, researchers propose a perplexity-differencing method to uncover finetuning objectives in model organisms. By generating completions from random prefills and ranking them by the difference in perplexity between finetuned and pre-finetuned models, they identify top-ranked outputs that reveal the intended or hidden training goals. This approach successfully exposes objectives across diverse models (N=70B parameters), including backdoored models and those trained to conceal behaviors, and remains effective even without access to the pre-finetuning checkpoint, using trusted reference models as substitutes. The method achieves state-of-the-art detection performance on AuditBench when integrated into an investigator agent.
为检测模型微调过程中引入的隐藏或有害行为，研究人员提出一种基于困惑度差值的方法，用于揭示模型生物的微调目标。通过从随机前缀生成补全内容，并按微调模型与微调前模型困惑度之差排序，该方法成功识别了70个参数规模从0.5到70B的模型生物的微调意图，包括后门模型和内化虚假事实的模型。该方法在合成文档微调或要求精确复现目标字符串的模型上表现尤为突出，且即使无微调前模型也可通过其他家族的可信模型替代。在AuditBench实验中，使用该方法的调查代理在检测隐藏行为上达到当前最优性能。
MMControl: Unified Multi-Modal Control for Joint Audio-Video Generation
Authors: Liyang Li, Wen Wang, Canyu Zhao, Tianjian Feng, Zhiyue Zhao, Hao Chen, Chunhua Shen
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-04-21T16:57:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:38:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026. Project page: https://aim-uofa.github.io/MMControl/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have enabled high-quality joint audio-video generation, producing videos with synchronized audio within a single model. However, existing controllable generation frameworks are typically restricted to video-only control. This restricts comprehensive controllability and often leads to suboptimal cross-modal alignment. To bridge this gap, we present MMControl, which enables users to perform Multi-Modal Control in joint audio-video generation. MMControl introduces a dual-stream conditional injection mechanism. It incorporates both visual and acoustic control signals, including reference images, reference audio, depth maps, and pose sequences, into a joint generation process. These conditions are injected through bypass branches into a joint audio-video Diffusion Transformer, enabling the model to simultaneously generate identity-consistent video and timbre-consistent audio under structural constraints. Furthermore, we introduce modality-specific guidance scaling, which allows users to independently and dynamically adjust the influence strength of each visual and acoustic condition at inference time. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MMControl achieves fine-grained, composable control over character identity, voice timbre, body pose, and scene layout in joint audio-video generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MMControl：用于联合音频-视频生成的统一多模态控制
扩散变换器（DiTs）的最新进展已实现高质量的联合音频-视频生成，单个模型可生成同步音频的视频。然而，现有可控生成框架通常仅限于视频控制，限制了全面可控性，常导致跨模态对齐不佳。为弥合此差距，我们提出MMControl，支持用户在联合音频-视频生成中进行多模态控制。MMControl引入双流条件注入机制，将视觉与听觉控制信号（如参考图像、参考音频、深度图和姿态序列）整合进联合生成过程。这些条件通过旁路分支注入联合音频-视频扩散变换器，使模型在结构约束下同时生成身份一致的视频和音色一致的音频。此外，我们引入模态特定引导缩放机制，允许用户在推理时独立动态调整每种视觉与听觉条件的影响强度。大量实验表明，MMControl在联合音频-视频生成中实现了对角色身份、音色、身体姿态和场景布局的精细、可组合控制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing controllable generation frameworks that only support video-based control, MMControl introduces a unified multi-modal control system for joint audio-video generation. It employs a dual-stream conditional injection mechanism that integrates visual and acoustic inputs—such as reference images, audio, depth maps, and pose sequences—into a joint Diffusion Transformer via bypass branches. This enables simultaneous generation of identity-consistent video and timbre-consistent audio under structural constraints. The method further supports modality-specific guidance scaling, allowing dynamic adjustment of control strength at inference time. Experimental results show MMControl achieves fine-grained, composable control over character identity, voice timbre, body pose, and scene layout, outperforming video-only baselines in cross-modal alignment and user-guided generation.
为解决现有可控生成框架仅支持视频控制、导致跨模态对齐不足的问题，MMControl提出统一的多模态控制方法用于联合音频-视频生成。其采用双流条件注入机制，将视觉与听觉输入（如参考图像、音频、深度图和姿态序列）通过旁路分支注入联合扩散Transformer，实现身份一致的视频与音色一致的音频同步生成。同时引入模态特定引导缩放机制，允许推理时动态调节各条件的影响强度。实验表明，MMControl在角色身份、音色、姿态和场景布局上实现了细粒度、可组合的控制，显著优于现有方法的跨模态对齐与可控性表现。
Learning from Mistakes: Rollout-Retrieval Lifelong Policy Learning for Autonomous Driving
Authors: Cheng Gong, Haoyang Wang, Chao Lu, Zirui Li, Jianwei Gong
First: 2026-06-29T16:37:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:37:50+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 6 figures. Code available at: https://github.com/Engibacter/R2LPL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Autonomous driving policies should be able to improve continually as deployment exposes them to increasingly diverse and long-tail traffic situations. However, most learning-based policies are trained or fine-tuned on expert demonstrations and then rely largely on generalization to handle challenging closed-loop scenarios, lacking an explicit mechanism to correct and retain the mistakes exposed in these scenarios. This paper studies autonomous driving policy improvement from a lifelong learning perspective: Can a pretrained policy improve continually by accumulating corrective knowledge derived from its own mistakes, while retaining previously acquired driving competence? To answer this question, we propose Rollout-Retrieval Lifelong Policy Learning (R$^2$LPL), a policy learning framework that retrieves corrective targets from recoverable policy-induced mistakes and retains the resulting knowledge through lifelong policy learning. R^2LPL addresses a key bottleneck in continual policy improvement: closed-loop mistakes reveal where the policy is weak, but do not directly specify what the policy should learn. By filtering recoverable mistake-related states and retrieving feasible corrective targets, R$^2$LPL turns sparse failure evidence into compact supervised knowledge for stable and sample-efficient policy improvement. We evaluate R$^2$LPL on large-scale closed-loop nuPlan benchmarks. With only a few rollout and continual-learning cycles, R$^2$LPL elevates a learning-based planner with moderate initial performance to state-of-the-art performance across the evaluated benchmarks, especially on the challenging and long-tail Test14-hard split. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of R$^2$LPL in converting recoverable closed-loop mistakes into corrective knowledge for sustained policy improvement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从错误中学习：面向自动驾驶的 rollout-检索终身策略学习
自动驾驶策略应能随着部署过程中暴露于日益多样且长尾的交通场景而持续改进。然而，大多数基于学习的策略通常在专家演示上训练或微调，然后主要依赖泛化能力应对挑战性的闭环场景，缺乏显式机制来纠正并保留这些场景中暴露的错误。本文从终身学习视角研究自动驾驶策略的持续改进：预训练策略能否通过积累自身错误所衍生的修正知识，同时保留已习得的驾驶能力，实现持续提升？为此，我们提出 Rollout-Retrieval Lifelong Policy Learning（R²LPL），一种策略学习框架，通过检索可恢复的策略诱导错误所对应的修正目标，并通过终身策略学习保留所获得的知识。R²LPL 解决了持续策略改进中的关键瓶颈：闭环错误揭示策略的薄弱环节，但并未直接指明策略应学习的内容。通过筛选可恢复的错误相关状态并检索可行的修正目标，R²LPL 将稀疏的失败证据转化为紧凑的监督知识，实现稳定且样本高效的策略改进。我们在大规模闭环 nuPlan 基准上评估 R²LPL。仅需少量 rollout 与持续学习循环，R²LPL 即可将初始性能中等的基于学习的规划器提升至各评估基准的最先进水平，尤其在具有挑战性的长尾 Test14-hard 分割上表现突出。结果表明，R²LPL 能有效将可恢复的闭环错误转化为修正知识，实现策略的持续改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for autonomous driving policies to continuously improve through real-world exposure to diverse and long-tail traffic scenarios, this paper introduces Rollout-Retrieval Lifelong Policy Learning (R^2LPL), a framework that enables policies to learn from their own mistakes. R^2LPL retrieves corrective targets from recoverable policy-induced failures and integrates this knowledge into lifelong learning to retain prior competence while improving. Evaluated on nuPlan benchmarks, R^2LPL significantly enhances a moderately performing baseline policy with minimal rollout and continual-learning cycles, achieving state-of-the-art performance—especially on the challenging Test14-hard split—demonstrating its efficacy in transforming sparse failure data into actionable, sample-efficient corrective knowledge.
为使自动驾驶策略能在实际部署中持续改进，本文提出Rollout-Retrieval Lifelong Policy Learning（R^2LPL）框架，该框架通过从可恢复的策略失误中检索修正目标，并结合终身学习机制保留先前能力以实现持续提升。在nuPlan基准测试中，R^2LPL仅需少量 rollout 与持续学习循环，便能将初始表现中等的策略提升至最先进水平，尤其在挑战性长尾Test14-hard子集上表现突出，验证了其将稀疏失败数据转化为高效修正知识的能力。
Orca: The World is in Your Mind
Authors: Yihao Wang, Yuheng Ji, Mingyu Cao, Yanqing Shen, Runze Xiao, Huaihai Lyu, Senwei Xie, Euan Liu, Klara Tian, Tianfeng Long, Yichi Zhang, Zhengliang Cai, Ruike Chen, Jifan Zhao, Ruochuan Shi, Zihan Tang, Jing Lyu, Wenxing Tan, Ningbo Zhang, Yangtao Hu, Yuming Gao, Xiansheng Chen, Junkai Zhao, Congsheng Xu, Boan Zhu, Ziqi Wang, Yupu Feng, Qiongqiong Zhang, Yingli Zhao, Yulong Ao, Shaoxuan Xie, You Liu, Guocai Yao, Leiduo Zhang, Xiaodan Liu, Yunyan Zhang, Yance Jiao, Xinyan Yang, Jiaxing Wei, Xu Liu, Tengfei Pan, Shaokai Nie, Chunlei Men, Sen Cui, Xiaojie Jin, Hongyang Li, Jianlan Luo, Yao Mu, Yunchao Wei, Jun Yan, Hang Zhao, Xiaolong Zheng, Jiaming Li, Yonghua Lin, Tiejun Huang, Zhongyuan Wang, Pengwei Wang
First: 2026-06-29T16:36:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:36:10+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://orca-wm.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce Orca, an initial instantiation of a general world foundation model. Orca learns a unified world latent space from multimodal world signals and exposes it through multimodal readout interfaces. Rather than optimizing isolated next-token, next-frame, or next-action prediction, we are centered on Next-State-Prediction modeling, offering a unified state-transition modeling route toward understanding, predicting, and acting upon the world. Orca learns through two complementary paradigms: unconscious learning captures dense natural state transitions from continuous videos, and conscious learning models sparse meaningful state transitions by language-described events and VQA supervision. For pre-training, we construct a large-scale world-learning inventory data, including 125K hours of video data and 160M event annotations. After pre-training, Orca learns a unified world latent space. To examine whether the learned latent supports downstream, we evaluate it by three representative downstream readouts: text generation, image prediction, and embodied action generation. Orca's backbone is frozen, and only the lightweight modality-specific decoders are trainable. Experiments show the scalability of the proposed paradigm and verify that stronger world latent enables stronger downstream readouts. Orca outperforms similar-sized specialized baselines. These results show that Orca, as a general world foundation model, presents a promising approach to understanding, predicting, and acting upon the world. Finally, we discuss the current limitations, aiming to provide useful insights and inspiration for the community.
中文标题/摘要
标题：orca：世界在你心中
我们介绍了orca，一个通用世界基础模型的初始实现。orca从多模态世界信号中学习统一的世界潜在空间，并通过多模态读出接口暴露该空间。与优化孤立的下一个token、下一帧或下一个动作预测不同，我们专注于状态预测建模，提供统一的状态转换建模路径，以理解、预测和应对世界。orca通过两种互补范式学习：无意识学习从连续视频中捕捉密集的自然状态转换；有意识学习通过语言描述的事件和视觉问答监督建模稀疏有意义的状态转换。在预训练阶段，我们构建了一个大规模世界学习数据集，包括12.5万小时的视频数据和1.6亿个事件标注。预训练后，orca学习到统一的世界潜在空间。为检验所学潜在空间是否支持下游任务，我们通过三个代表性下游读出任务进行评估：文本生成、图像预测和具身动作生成。orca的主干网络被冻结，仅轻量级模态特定解码器可训练。实验表明该范式具有可扩展性，并验证更强的世界潜在空间可带来更强的下游读出能力。orca在相似规模的专用基线模型上表现更优。这些结果表明，作为通用世界基础模型，orca为理解、预测和应对世界提供了一种有前景的方法。最后，我们讨论了当前局限性，旨在为社区提供有益的见解和启发。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a unified framework to understand, predict, and act in the world, Orca introduces a general world foundation model that learns a shared latent space from multimodal signals. It employs Next-State-Prediction as its core modeling objective, combining unconscious learning from continuous video streams and conscious learning via language-guided events and VQA supervision. Pre-trained on a large-scale dataset of 125K hours of video and 160M event annotations, Orca demonstrates strong downstream performance in text generation, image prediction, and embodied action generation, even with only lightweight decoders trainable while freezing its backbone. Results show that Orca outperforms comparable specialized models, validating the scalability and effectiveness of its world latent space approach.
为实现对世界统一的理解、预测与行动，Orca提出一种通用世界基础模型，通过多模态信号学习共享的潜在空间。其核心建模目标为状态预测，结合从连续视频中学习的无意识学习与通过语言事件和视觉问答监督的有意识学习。在包含12.5万小时视频和1.6亿事件标注的大规模数据集上预训练后，Orca在文本生成、图像预测和具身动作生成等下游任务中表现优异，仅需轻量级解码器可训练而冻结主干。实验表明，其世界潜在空间越强，下游性能越优，且优于同等规模的专用模型，验证了该范式的有效性与可扩展性。
Entity Binding Failures in Tool-Augmented Agents
Authors: Rahul Suresh Babu, Shashank Indukuri
First: 2026-06-29T16:34:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:34:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tool-augmented language-model agents are often evaluated by whether they select the correct tool, produce valid API arguments, and complete the requested task. However, an agent may choose the right tool and still act on the wrong external entity. For example, a request to "email Alex about the launch" may lead the agent to contact the wrong Alex, attach the wrong launch document, reply in the wrong thread, or update the wrong customer account. We call these errors entity binding failures. This paper studies entity binding failures as a distinct reliability and safety problem in tool-augmented agents. We formalize the separation between tool correctness and entity correctness, introduce a taxonomy of wrong-entity failures in enterprise workflows, and evaluate entity-aware execution mechanisms including entity-resolution preconditions, confidence-gated binding, clarification under ambiguity, and provenance tracking. In a controlled diagnostic evaluation across 60 tasks, five model backends, and six tool-use methods, all methods achieved 0.0 percent wrong-tool error, yet action-oriented baselines still produced wrong-entity actions in 24.0-26.0 percent of runs. Entity-aware methods eliminated wrong-entity actions and risk-weighted wrong-entity exposure in this setting, but reduced direct task completion by deferring under ambiguity. These findings show that safe tool use requires not only selecting the correct tool, but also reliably binding natural-language references to the correct real-world entity before action.
中文标题/摘要
标题：工具增强型代理中的实体绑定失败
工具增强型语言模型代理通常通过是否选择正确工具、生成有效API参数并完成请求任务来评估。然而，代理可能选对工具但仍作用于错误的外部实体。例如，请求“给Alex发邮件关于产品发布”可能导致代理联系错误的Alex、附加错误的发布文档、回复错误的对话线程或更新错误的客户账户。我们将这类错误称为实体绑定失败。本文将实体绑定失败视为工具增强型代理中独立的可靠性与安全问题，形式化了工具正确性与实体正确性的分离，提出了企业工作流中错误实体失败的分类体系，并评估了包括实体解析前置条件、置信度门控绑定、歧义下的澄清以及溯源追踪在内的实体感知执行机制。在涵盖60项任务、五种模型后端和六种工具使用方法的受控诊断评估中，所有方法均实现0.0%的错误工具率，但以行动为导向的基线方法仍导致24.0%-26.0%的运行中出现错误实体操作。实体感知方法在此设置中消除了错误实体操作及风险加权的错误实体暴露，但因在歧义时延迟执行而降低了直接任务完成率。这些发现表明，安全使用工具不仅需要选择正确工具，还需在执行前可靠地将自然语言引用绑定至正确现实实体。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the overlooked issue of entity binding failures in tool-augmented agents, where agents may select the correct tool but act on the wrong real-world entity, such as contacting the wrong person or updating the wrong record. The authors formalize the distinction between tool correctness and entity correctness, propose a taxonomy of such failures in enterprise workflows, and evaluate four entity-aware mechanisms: entity-resolution preconditions, confidence-gated binding, clarification under ambiguity, and provenance tracking. In a controlled evaluation across 60 tasks, five models, and six tool-use methods, all methods achieved zero wrong-tool errors, but traditional action-oriented baselines still produced wrong-entity actions in 24–26% of runs. Entity-aware methods successfully eliminated wrong-entity actions and reduced risk-weighted exposure, albeit at the cost of slightly lower task completion rates due to deferral under ambiguity, demonstrating that safe tool use requires reliable entity binding in addition to correct tool selection.
该论文研究了工具增强型代理中常见的实体绑定失败问题，即代理可能选对工具但作用于错误的现实实体，如联系错误的人或更新错误的记录。作者明确区分了工具正确性与实体正确性，提出企业工作流中错误实体的分类体系，并评估了四种实体感知机制：实体解析前置条件、置信度门控绑定、模糊时澄清和溯源追踪。在覆盖60项任务、五种模型和六种工具使用方法的控制实验中，所有机制均实现零错误工具选择，但基础行动导向方法仍导致24–26%的错误实体操作。实体感知机制成功消除了错误实体操作并降低了风险暴露，但因在模糊时延迟执行，任务完成率略有下降。
$μ$Flow: Leveraging Average Images for Improving Generalisation of Deepfake Faces Detectors
Authors: Orazio Pontorno, Mattia Litrico, Luca Guarnera, Mario Valerio Giuffrida, Sebastiano Battiato
Venue: ECCV
First: 2026-06-29T16:31:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:31:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the European Conference on Computer Vision (ECCV) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Current generative models, including GANs and diffusion models, have reached an outstanding level of photorealism, posing significant risks to privacy and security. To ensure real-world applicability, deepfake detectors must generalise effectively to unseen generators. However, most existing approaches rely on supervised training with both real and fake images, which limits their generalisation especially across generators categories (e.g. GANs vs DMs). In this work, we introduce $μ$Flow, a one-class deepfake detector trained only on real images without relying on pseudo-deepfakes or synthetic artifacts. Our approach builds on the observation that averaging multiple images amplifies consistent generative traces, producing highly discriminative feature representations. We leverage this property by modelling the distribution of features extracted from averaged images and training a normalizing flow to align the feature space of individual images with this distribution. This alignment yields a likelihood-based criterion that separates real and fake samples while promoting strong generalisation. We evaluate $μ$Flow on a fully out-of-distribution setting, where both real and fake datasets are unseen during training. Experimental results show that our method significantly outperforms SOTA detectors. Project page: https://opontorno.github.io/MuFlow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：μFlow：利用平均图像提升深度伪造人脸检测器的泛化能力
当前生成模型（包括GAN和扩散模型）已达到极高的照片真实度，对隐私与安全构成重大威胁。为确保实际应用，深度伪造检测器必须能有效泛化至未见过的生成器。然而，多数现有方法依赖于真实与伪造图像的监督训练，尤其在跨生成器类别（如GAN与DM）时泛化能力受限。本文提出μFlow，一种仅使用真实图像训练的单类深度伪造检测器，无需伪深度伪造或合成伪影。我们的方法基于观察：对多张图像取平均可放大一致的生成痕迹，产生高度判别性的特征表示。我们利用该特性，建模平均图像提取特征的分布，并训练归一化流以对齐单张图像的特征空间与该分布。该对齐产生基于似然的判别准则，有效分离真实与伪造样本，同时促进强泛化能力。我们在完全分布外设置下评估μFlow，训练时真实与伪造数据均未见过。实验结果表明，本方法显著优于当前最先进检测器。项目页面：https://opontorno.github.io/MuFlow。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for deepfake detectors that generalize across unseen generative models, this work introduces $μ$Flow, a one-class detector trained solely on real images without synthetic data. It leverages the observation that averaging multiple images enhances consistent generative artifacts, enabling discriminative feature extraction. By modeling the distribution of features from averaged images and training a normalizing flow to align individual image features with this distribution, $μ$Flow derives a likelihood-based criterion to distinguish real from fake samples. Evaluated in a fully out-of-distribution setting, $μ$Flow significantly outperforms state-of-the-art detectors, demonstrating strong generalization across generator types.
为提升深度伪造检测器对未见过的生成器的泛化能力，本文提出$μ$Flow，一种仅使用真实图像训练的单类检测器，无需合成数据。该方法利用多图像平均能增强生成器一致痕迹的特性，提取具有判别力的特征。通过建模平均图像的特征分布，并训练归一化流将单张图像的特征对齐至该分布，构建基于似然的判别准则区分真实与伪造图像。在完全分布外测试中，$μ$Flow显著优于现有最先进方法，展现出对不同生成器类型的强泛化能力。
ITSPACE: Monotone Gaussian Optimal Transport Updates
Authors: Woojoo Na, Jennifer Dy
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-29T16:29:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:29:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026. Camera-ready version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Covariance matrices serve as compact descriptors of feature distributions in many machine-learning pipelines, including domain adaptation and Gaussian embeddings. Under a centered Gaussian approximation, the unregularized Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport (OT) discrepancy admits a closed form on covariances given by the Bures-Wasserstein (BW) objective on the symmetric positive definite (SPD) cone. We propose ITSPACE (Iterative Transport for Stable Proximal Alignment of Covariance Embeddings), a proximal majorization-minimization method that directly optimizes this exact BW objective through closed-form updates in a square-root factorization. In exact arithmetic, each iteration satisfies a sufficient-decrease inequality for the BW objective; under inexact polar computations, we provide an explicit certificate-gap bound controlling deviations from exact descent. The resulting iterations preserve PSD structure by construction and naturally support rank-restricted factors, making ITSPACE well-suited as a lightweight inner-loop primitive in settings where adaptation must be performed from unlabeled target batches under strict step and compute budgets. Across real-world covariance-alignment benchmarks, ITSPACE reaches low-BW-gap solutions substantially faster than BW-gradient descent, methods based on other covariance geometries, and entropically regularized sample-OT baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ITSPACE：单调高斯最优传输更新
协方差矩阵在许多机器学习流程（包括领域自适应和高斯嵌入）中作为特征分布的紧凑描述符。在中心高斯近似下，未正则化的Wasserstein-2最优传输（OT）差异在协方差上具有闭式解，即对称正定（SPD）锥上的Bures-Wasserstein（BW）目标。我们提出ITSPACE（用于协方差嵌入稳定近端对齐的迭代传输），这是一种近端主化最小化方法，通过平方根因子分解的闭式更新直接优化该精确的BW目标。在精确算术下，每次迭代满足BW目标的充分下降不等式；在不精确极分解计算下，我们提供显式的证书间隙界，控制与精确下降的偏差。所得迭代通过构造保持正定结构，并自然支持秩受限因子，使ITSPACE非常适合在标注目标批次受限于严格步数和计算预算的场景中作为轻量级内循环原语。在现实世界协方差对齐基准测试中，ITSPACE比BW梯度下降、基于其他协方差几何的方法以及熵正则化样本OT基线显著更快地达到低BW间隙解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient covariance alignment in machine learning tasks like domain adaptation, this paper introduces ITSPACE, a proximal majorization-minimization algorithm that directly optimizes the Bures-Wasserstein objective over symmetric positive definite matrices via closed-form updates in square-root factorization. The method guarantees sufficient decrease in exact arithmetic and provides explicit error bounds under inexact polar computations, while inherently preserving positive semi-definite structure and supporting rank-restricted factors. Experimental results show ITSPACE converges to low-BW-gap solutions significantly faster than gradient descent on the BW objective, alternative covariance geometry methods, and entropically regularized sample-OT baselines across real-world benchmarks.
为了解决机器学习任务（如领域自适应）中协方差对齐的效率问题，本文提出了ITSPACE算法，这是一种基于近端极大化-最小化框架的方法，通过平方根因子分解的闭式更新直接优化Bures-Wasserstein目标函数。该方法在精确计算下保证目标函数的充分下降，并在近似极分解下提供明确的误差界，同时天然保持正定性并支持秩受限因子。实验表明，ITSPACE在多个真实世界基准上比梯度法、其他协方差几何方法及熵正则化样本最优传输基线更快收敛至低BW差距解。
Staged Hybridisation for Visual Quantum Reinforcement Learning via Knowledge Distillation
Authors: Javier Lazaro, Juan-Ignacio Vazquez, Pablo Garcia-Bringas
First: 2026-06-29T16:28:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:28:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual environments are a demanding setting for quantum reinforcement learning (QRL): high-dimensional observations, unstable RL optimisation, and constrained variational quantum circuits (VQCs) are difficult to train jointly. This paper studies knowledge distillation (KD) as a staged hybridisation strategy for visual QRL. Instead of training a hybrid visual agent end-to-end from pixels, we first train a classical visual teacher, freeze its encoder as a feature interface, and distil the teacher's policy behaviour into compact downstream heads. These heads can be classical or VQC-based, enabling small quantum-compatible students to be evaluated under the same frozen representation as compact classical controls. We evaluate the pipeline on CartPole Pixels and Acrobot Pixels. The results show that staged KD enables shallow VQC heads to acquire non-trivial visual-control behaviour in settings where direct pixel-based training would be substantially more difficult. Angle-encoded VQC heads retain near-teacher performance, while amplitude-encoded heads push compactness to an extreme regime, at the cost of greater fragility, stronger budget sensitivity, and higher simulation time. Overall, staged KD reframes visual QRL as a compact-head learning problem, opening a practical route for training small quantum-compatible policies outside the standard end-to-end RL loop.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过知识蒸馏的分阶段混合化实现视觉量子强化学习
视觉环境对量子强化学习（QRL）提出了严峻挑战：高维观测、不稳定的强化学习优化以及受限的变分量子电路（VQCs）难以联合训练。本文将知识蒸馏（KD）作为视觉QRL的分阶段混合化策略。我们不直接从像素端到端训练混合视觉智能体，而是先训练一个经典视觉教师模型，冻结其编码器作为特征接口，并将教师的策略行为蒸馏到紧凑的下游头部。这些头部可以是经典或基于VQC的，从而允许小型量子兼容的学生模型在与紧凑经典控制相同的冻结表示下进行评估。 我们在CartPole Pixels和Acrobot Pixels上评估该流程。结果表明，分阶段KD使浅层VQC头部能够在直接像素训练极为困难的环境中获得非平凡的视觉控制行为。角度编码的VQC头部保持接近教师的性能，而幅度编码的头部则将紧凑性推向极端，但代价是更大的脆弱性、更强的预算敏感性和更高的仿真时间。总体而言，分阶段KD将视觉QRL重新定义为紧凑头部学习问题，为在标准端到端强化学习循环之外训练小型量子兼容策略开辟了实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of training quantum reinforcement learning agents in high-dimensional visual environments, this work proposes staged knowledge distillation as a hybridisation strategy. It first trains a classical visual teacher, freezes its encoder, and distills its policy into compact downstream heads—either classical or quantum circuit-based—allowing small quantum-compatible agents to learn under a shared, stable feature representation. Experiments on CartPole Pixels and Acrobot Pixels show that this approach enables shallow quantum circuit heads to achieve non-trivial visual control, even where direct pixel training fails. Angle-encoded circuits maintain near-teacher performance, while amplitude-encoded circuits achieve extreme compactness but at the cost of fragility and higher resource demands.
为应对视觉环境下量子强化学习训练困难的问题，本文提出分阶段知识蒸馏策略。首先训练一个经典视觉教师模型，冻结其编码器作为特征接口，再将教师策略蒸馏至紧凑的下游头（可为经典或量子电路），使小型量子兼容代理能在稳定特征表示下学习。在CartPole Pixels和Acrobot Pixels实验中，该方法使浅层量子电路头能实现非平凡视觉控制，角度编码头性能接近教师，而振幅编码头虽极度紧凑，但更脆弱、更敏感且需更高模拟开销。
Regime-Aware Peer Specialization for Robust RAG under Heterogeneous Knowledge Conflicts
Authors: Bo Wang, Heyan Huang, Yaolin Li, Yanghao Zhou, Jiahao Teng, Ziyi Yang, Ge Shi, Chong Feng
First: 2026-06-29T16:25:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:25:13+00:00
Comments: Working in Progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language models by grounding generation in external context. However, it can be fragile when the retrieved context conflicts with the model's parametric knowledge. Such conflicts span a reliability spectrum, ranging from reliable and partially reliable evidence to adversarial context. Existing remedies often handle such heterogeneous conflicts with regime-agnostic supervision, which can conflate incompatible learning signals across reliability regimes. To disentangle these signals, we propose RAPS-DA, a regime-aware peer specialization framework that addresses conflict at two complementary granularities. At the sample level, conflicts are divided into three regimes, including Grounding, Arbitration, and Resistance, with one same-scale peer specialist trained per regime from a shared base model. Each sample is then hard-routed to its regime-matched peer for on-policy reverse-KL supervision. At the token level, a dual-layer selector uses inter-teacher disagreement, student-teacher divergence, and student entropy to filter uninformative or unstable tokens, upweight confidently misaligned ones, and gradually focus supervision on high-conflict tokens as the student matures. Gains stem from specialization at a fixed model scale, not from a stronger teacher, and the peer specialists exist only during training, so the deployed student requires no regime labels or peer access. Experiments on five conflict scenarios and two out-of-distribution benchmarks show RAPS-DA surpasses all prompting, decoding, fine-tuning, RL, and single-teacher baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向异质知识冲突的鲁棒RAG的制度感知同伴专业化
检索增强生成（RAG）通过将生成与外部上下文相结合来提升语言模型性能。然而，当检索到的上下文与模型参数知识发生冲突时，RAG可能变得脆弱。此类冲突涵盖可靠性谱系，从可靠和部分可靠证据到对抗性上下文。现有解决方案常采用制度无关监督处理此类异质冲突，可能导致不同可靠性制度间不兼容的学习信号混杂。为分离这些信号，我们提出RAPS-DA，一种制度感知的同伴专业化框架，从两个互补粒度解决冲突。在样本层面，冲突划分为三个制度：接地、仲裁与抵抗，每个制度从共享基础模型训练一个同规模同伴专家。每个样本随后被硬路由至匹配制度的同伴专家，接受基于策略的逆KL监督。在令牌层面，双层选择器利用教师间分歧、学生-教师差异及学生熵值，过滤无信息或不稳定令牌，强化自信错位令牌权重，并随着学生成熟逐步聚焦高冲突令牌。性能提升源于固定模型规模下的专业化，而非更强教师，且同伴专家仅存在于训练阶段，部署的学生无需制度标签或同伴访问。在五个冲突场景和两个分布外基准测试中，RAPS-DA超越所有提示、解码、微调、强化学习及单教师基线。
Summary / 总结
To address the fragility of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) when external context conflicts with model knowledge, the paper proposes RAPS-DA, a regime-aware peer specialization framework that disentangles heterogeneous conflict signals by dividing them into three reliability regimes: Grounding, Arbitration, and Resistance. Each regime is handled by a dedicated peer specialist trained from a shared base model, with samples hard-routed to their matching peer for on-policy reverse-KL supervision. At the token level, a dual-layer selector filters tokens based on inter-teacher disagreement, student-teacher divergence, and entropy, prioritizing high-conflict tokens as training progresses. Experiments across five conflict scenarios and two out-of-distribution benchmarks demonstrate that RAPS-DA outperforms all baseline methods—including prompting, fine-tuning, RL, and single-teacher approaches—without requiring regime labels or peer access at inference time.
为解决RAG在外部检索内容与模型知识冲突时的脆弱性，本文提出RAPS-DA框架，通过将冲突划分为Grounding、Arbitration和Resistance三个阶段，为每个阶段训练专属的同伴专家。样本被路由至匹配的同伴专家进行基于策略的反KL监督。在词级，双层选择器依据教师间分歧、学生-教师差异和熵值过滤无信息或不稳定词元，优先强化高冲突词元。实验在五个冲突场景和两个分布外基准上表明，RAPS-DA超越所有基线方法（包括提示、微调和强化学习），且无需推理时的阶段标签或同伴访问。
HASTE: A Framework for Training-Free, Dynamic, and Steerable Compression of Pre-Trained Convolutional Neural Networks
Authors: Lukas Meiner, Jens Mehnert, Alexandru Paul Condurache
Venue: Springer Nature Computer Science, Volume 7, Issue 6, Article 611, 2026
First: 2026-06-29T16:24:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:24:17+00:00
Comments: This preprint has not undergone peer review or any post-submission improvements or corrections. The Version of Record of this article is published in Springer Nature Compute Science, and is available online at https://doi.org/10.1007/s42979-026-05177-0
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deploying large convolutional neural networks (CNNs) on resource-constrained devices is challenging due to their high computational cost. While dynamic execution methods are promising, existing approaches for CNNs typically require specialized training or fine-tuning, limiting their effectiveness when applied to pre-trained models and requiring data access. To address this gap, we propose HASTE (Hashing for Tractable Efficiency), a plug-and-play convolution module that enables training-free, dynamic compression of large pre-trained CNNs. At inference time, HASTE uses locality-sensitive hashing to identify and merge redundant channels of latent feature maps on a patch-wise basis. This process simultaneously compresses the depth of both input features and their corresponding filters, resulting in computationally cheaper convolutions. We conduct extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet across a range of architectures, demonstrating a 46.2% FLOPs reduction in a ResNet34 on CIFAR-10 with only a 1.25% drop in accuracy, without any retraining. We support our claims by comprehensive ablation studies to validate our core design choices, an analysis of the method's properties and limitations, and a discussion that connects our channel merging scheme to the conceptually related task of token merging in Vision Transformers. Our results demonstrate that HASTE provides an effective solution for steerable compression of pre-trained CNNs at runtime, opening new possibilities for the deployment of efficient deep learning methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HASTE：一种用于预训练卷积神经网络无训练、动态且可调控压缩的框架
在资源受限设备上部署大型卷积神经网络（CNN）因计算成本高而具有挑战性。虽然动态执行方法前景广阔，但现有CNN压缩方法通常需要专门训练或微调，限制了其在预训练模型上的应用，且需访问数据。为解决此问题，我们提出HASTE（用于可处理效率的哈希），一种即插即用的卷积模块，可实现对大型预训练CNN的无训练动态压缩。推理时，HASTE利用局部敏感哈希在块级识别并合并冗余的特征图通道，同时压缩输入特征及其对应滤波器的深度，从而降低卷积计算成本。我们在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上对多种架构进行广泛实验，结果表明在CIFAR-10的ResNet34模型中，FLOPs减少46.2%，准确率仅下降1.25%，无需重新训练。我们通过全面的消融研究验证核心设计选择，分析方法特性与局限性，并讨论其通道合并方案与视觉Transformer中相关任务——Token合并的联系。结果表明，HASTE为预训练CNN在运行时提供了一种有效的可调控压缩方案，为高效深度学习方法的部署开辟了新可能。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational burden of deploying large CNNs on resource-limited devices without retraining, HASTE introduces a training-free, dynamic compression framework that operates at inference time. It leverages locality-sensitive hashing to identify and merge redundant channels in latent feature maps on a patch-by-patch basis, simultaneously compressing input feature depth and corresponding convolutional filters. Evaluated on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet across multiple architectures, HASTE achieves a 46.2% reduction in FLOPs for ResNet34 on CIFAR-10 with only a 1.25% accuracy drop, without requiring any fine-tuning or data access. Ablation studies and theoretical analysis confirm the effectiveness of its channel merging strategy and its conceptual alignment with token merging in Vision Transformers.
为解决在资源受限设备上部署大型CNN时的计算负担且无需再训练，HASTE提出一种动态压缩框架，在推理阶段通过局部敏感哈希识别并合并特征图中的冗余通道，同时压缩输入特征深度和对应卷积核。在CIFAR-10和ImageNet多个架构上实验表明，其在ResNet34上实现46.2%的FLOPs降低，仅牺牲1.25%精度，通过消融实验和理论分析验证其设计，并关联到视觉Transformer中的token合并概念。
Surrogate Modeling for Explainable Predictive Time Series Corrections
Authors: Alfredo Lopez, Florian Sobieczky
Venue: A. López and F. Sobieczky, Surrogate Modeling for Explainable Predictive Time Series Corrections. Quality and Reliability Engineering International (2026)
First: 2024-12-27T19:17:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:24:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce a local surrogate approach for explainable time-series forecasting. An initially non-interpretable predictive model to improve the forecast of a classical time-series 'base model' is used. 'Explainability' of the correction is provided by fitting the base model again to the data from which the error prediction is removed (subtracted), yielding a difference in the model parameters which can be interpreted. We provide illustrative examples to demonstrate the potential of the method to discover and explain underlying patterns in the data.
