我们提出了一种局部代理方法，用于可解释的时间序列预测。该方法首先使用一个原本不可解释的预测模型来改进经典时间序列‘基模型’的预测效果。通过将基模型再次拟合到已去除误差预测值的数据上，从而获得模型参数的变化，这种变化可以被解释，从而实现校正的可解释性。我们通过示例展示了该方法发现和解释数据中潜在模式的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable corrections in time-series forecasting, this paper proposes a local surrogate method that enhances a classical base model by fitting it to error-corrected data. The correction’s explainability arises from comparing model parameters before and after subtracting predicted errors, revealing interpretable differences that reflect underlying data patterns. Experimental results demonstrate the method’s ability to uncover and explain such patterns in illustrative cases, validating its potential for improving both accuracy and interpretability in forecasting.
为提升时间序列预测的可解释性，本文提出一种局部代理方法，通过将基础模型重新拟合到已减去预测误差的数据上，从而获得可解释的参数差异以解释修正效果。通过示例展示，该方法能够揭示原始模型忽略的数据内在模式，证明其在发现和解释时间序列结构变化方面的潜力。
3D Scene-Adaptive Trajectory-Controllable Human Image Animation with Camera Movement
Authors: Deyin Liu, Jicheng Xu, Lin Yuanbo Wu, Xiaowei Zhao, Xiatian Zhu, Zhe Jin, Anjan Dutta
First: 2026-06-29T16:22:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:22:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Human image animation, which aims to generate a video of a reference subject following a provided action sequence, has received increasing research interest. With the development of diffusion-based/flow-based video foundation models, existing animation works have began to upgrade the guidance information from 2D skeleton/pose to 3D modeling conditions. Despite achieving reasonable results, these approaches face challenges in synthesizing trajectory-controllable human motion within natural scene under changed camera views. In this work, we present a scene-adaptive human image animation framework that controls both human motion and camera trajectories within a reconstructed 3D environment for video generation. To achieve this, we first develop a ground-adaptive 3D motion retargeting approach to enable user-friendly motion trajectory control adapting to the changes of elevations of ground and orientations automatically. Then we design a viewpoint-adaptive latent fusion mechanism to inject point-cloud geometric priors through scene-visibility masking into the generative process, providing precise guidance of viewpoint changes under camera control. Experiments on two standard human image animation benchmark datasets demonstrate remarkable improvements of our method over the state of the arts in related video generation metics. Project page: https://robinhood256100.github.io/web-disp

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more natural and controllable human animation in dynamic 3D scenes under varying camera perspectives, this work introduces a scene-adaptive framework that jointly controls human motion and camera trajectory within a reconstructed 3D environment. The method first employs a ground-adaptive 3D motion retargeting technique to automatically adjust motion paths according to terrain elevation and orientation, enabling intuitive trajectory control. It then integrates a viewpoint-adaptive latent fusion mechanism that incorporates point-cloud geometric priors via scene-visibility masking to guide camera-aware motion synthesis. Experimental results on two benchmark datasets show significant performance gains over existing methods in video generation metrics, demonstrating improved realism and trajectory fidelity under complex scene and camera conditions.
为实现更自然且可控的人体动画，特别是在变化的相机视角和复杂场景下，本文提出一种场景自适应框架，联合控制人体运动与相机轨迹。方法首先采用地面自适应3D运动重定向技术，自动调整运动路径以适应地形高度和朝向变化，提升用户操作的直观性；随后设计视角自适应潜在融合机制，通过场景可见性掩码注入点云几何先验，引导相机控制下的运动生成。在两个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在视频生成指标上显著优于现有方法，展现出更强的轨迹控制能力和场景一致性。
Informational Frustration in Neural Manifolds: Shannon Bottlenecks and the Limits of Learnability
Authors: Srinivasa Rao P., Vangmayi P Reddy
First: 2026-06-29T16:20:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:20:02+00:00
Comments: 8
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Why overparameterised deep networks generalise so remarkably well remains one of the most stubborn open questions in machine learning theory. Classical frameworks like VC dimension and Rademacher complexity predict catastrophic overfitting in modern models, leaving a massive theoretical gap between theory and reality. In this paper, we bridge this divide by introducing a unified framework that links information theory, topology, and statistical mechanics to map the hard limits of deep learning. Central to our approach is the Entropic Learnability Horizon (ELH): a fundamental law stating that a network can only truly learn a target function if the Shannon entropy of the data manifold outpaces the topological entropy of the function's decision boundary, balanced by the von Neumann entropy of the network's weight space. We establish the Shannon-Topological Bottleneck Theorem, proving that when a target boundary's geometric complexity exceeds this informational horizon, the system undergoes a sudden entropic phase transition. It falls into a state of Informational Frustration - a glassy, rigid memorization phase where generalization becomes thermodynamically impossible. Using this lens, we show that the enigmatic phenomenon of "grokking" is actually an Entropic Release, where weights abruptly reorganise to unlock the bottleneck. Finally, we translate this theory into practice with Entropic Gradient Descent (EGD), an optimization algorithm that dynamically manages weight entropy to keep learning on track. Ultimately, this work repositions entropy not just as a tool for tracking uncertainty but as the fundamental physical currency that dictates whether a machine can learn.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经流形中的信息挫败：香农瓶颈与可学习性的极限
为什么过度参数化的深度网络能如此惊人地泛化，仍是机器学习理论中最顽固的开放问题之一。经典框架如VC维和Rademacher复杂度预测现代模型会灾难性过拟合，导致理论与现实之间存在巨大鸿沟。本文通过引入一个统一框架，将信息论、拓扑学和统计力学相结合，以映射深度学习的硬性极限。我们的核心方法是“熵学习地平线”（ELH）：一个基本定律，指出网络仅当数据流形的香农熵超过目标函数决策边界的拓扑熵（并由网络权重空间的冯·诺依曼熵平衡）时，才能真正学习该目标函数。我们建立了香农-拓扑瓶颈定理，证明当目标边界的几何复杂度超过此信息地平线时，系统将经历突然的熵相变，进入“信息挫败”状态——一种玻璃态、刚性记忆化阶段，泛化在热力学上变得不可能。通过这一视角，我们表明“grokking”这一神秘现象实际上是“熵释放”，即权重突然重组以突破瓶颈。最后，我们将该理论转化为实践，提出“熵梯度下降”（EGD）优化算法，动态管理权重熵以保持学习进程。最终，本工作将熵重新定位，不仅作为追踪不确定性的工具，更作为决定机器能否学习的根本物理货币。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the persistent theoretical gap between classical learning bounds and the empirical success of overparameterized deep networks, proposing a unified framework that integrates information theory, topology, and statistical mechanics. The core contribution is the Entropic Learnability Horizon (ELH), a principle stating that learnability requires the Shannon entropy of the data manifold to exceed the topological entropy of the target function’s decision boundary, modulated by the von Neumann entropy of the weight space. When this threshold is breached, the system enters an 'Informational Frustration' phase—a glassy, memorization-dominated state where generalization fails due to entropic phase transition. The authors link this to the phenomenon of 'grokking' as an 'Entropic Release,' where weight reorganization unlocks the bottleneck. They further propose Entropic Gradient Descent (EGD), an optimization method that dynamically controls weight entropy to sustain learning, demonstrating that entropy is not merely a measure of uncertainty but the fundamental physical constraint governing learnability.
本文旨在弥合经典学习理论与过参数化深度网络卓越泛化能力之间的理论鸿沟，提出一个融合信息论、拓扑学与统计物理的统一框架。核心概念是“熵学习极限”（ELH），即网络仅在数据流形的香农熵超过决策边界的拓扑熵（并受权重空间冯·诺依曼熵调节）时才能真正学习目标函数。一旦该阈值被突破，系统将经历熵相变，陷入“信息挫败”状态——表现为僵化记忆与泛化热力学不可行。作者指出，“grokking”现象实为熵释放，即权重重组解除瓶颈，并提出“熵梯度下降”（EGD）算法，通过动态调控权重熵维持学习进程。
High-Resolution Flood Mapping With Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 via Misalignment-Robust Cross-Sensor Learning and Generative Despeckling
Authors: David Ma, Jeremy Feinstein, Shreya Pandit, Arkaprabha Ganguli, Eugene Yan
First: 2026-06-29T16:19:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:19:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable high-resolution flood extent mapping from satellite imagery remains constrained by limited data fidelity and sensor-specific artifacts. Multispectral optical imagery is degraded by clouds, shadows, and urban confounders, while synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery is affected by speckle noise and sensor co-registration uncertainty. This work presents an integrated flood mapping framework that jointly addresses these limitations through curated datasets and novel learning strategies. We introduce a new Sentinel-2 (S2) and Sentinel-1 (S1) dataset covering the contiguous United States, featuring pixel-accurate 10 m water masks with emphasis on challenging weather conditions and urban environments that are underrepresented in existing benchmarks. High-quality S2 annotations are manually produced using rigorous geospatial labeling protocols and transferred to SAR imagery through weakly labeled temporally coincident acquisitions. To address SAR-specific artifacts, a shift-invariant loss function is employed to tolerate residual geolocation uncertainty between SAR imagery and optical-derived labels, and a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE) is trained on multitemporal SAR composites to suppress speckle while preserving flood-relevant spatial structure. Experiments using UNet and UNet++ architectures demonstrate strong multispectral performance (AUPRC up to 0.956) and statistically significant improvements in SAR flood mapping when using shift-invariant loss and CVAE-based despeckling compared to classical filters. These results underscore the importance of dataset fidelity, misalignment-robust training, and demonstrate the viability of generative despeckling for operational flood mapping.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过抗错位跨传感器学习与生成式去斑点技术实现高分辨率洪水制图（Sentinel-1与Sentinel-2）
从卫星影像可靠地进行高分辨率洪水范围制图仍受限于数据保真度不足和传感器特有伪影。多光谱光学影像受云层、阴影和城市干扰影响，而合成孔径雷达（SAR）影像则受斑点噪声和传感器配准不确定性影响。本文提出一个集成式洪水制图框架，通过精选数据集和新颖学习策略共同解决上述限制。我们构建了一个新的覆盖美国本土的Sentinel-2（S2）与Sentinel-1（S1）数据集，包含像素级精确的10米水体掩膜，重点涵盖现有基准中代表性不足的恶劣天气和城市环境。高质量S2标注通过严格的地理空间标注协议人工生成，并通过弱标注的时相一致SAR影像迁移至SAR数据。为应对SAR特有伪影，采用移位不变损失函数容忍SAR影像与光学衍生标签间的残余地理定位不确定性，并训练条件变分自编码器（CVAE）处理多时相SAR复合影像以抑制斑点同时保留洪水相关空间结构。实验表明，使用UNet和UNet++架构时，多光谱性能优异（AUPRC高达0.956），且在采用移位不变损失和CVAE去斑点后，SAR洪水制图效果相比传统滤波方法有统计显著提升。结果强调了数据集保真度与抗错位训练的重要性，并验证了生成式去斑点在业务化洪水制图中的可行性。

This study addresses the challenge of accurate high-resolution flood mapping by combining Sentinel-2 optical and Sentinel-1 SAR imagery, which suffer from cloud cover, speckle noise, and misalignment. The authors construct a new dataset with pixel-accurate 10 m water masks across the U.S., focusing on urban and cloudy conditions. They train a flood mapping model using UNet and UNet++ architectures, incorporating a shift-invariant loss to handle geolocation uncertainty and a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE) to despeckle SAR data while preserving flood structure. Experiments show the method achieves AUPRC up to 0.956 on multispectral data and statistically significant improvements over classical filters when using the proposed techniques.
该研究旨在解决高分辨率洪水制图中光学与SAR影像存在的云遮挡、阴影、城市干扰及斑点噪声等限制。作者构建了覆盖美国本土的Sentinel-2与Sentinel-1新数据集，标注了像素级10米水体掩膜，重点涵盖现有基准数据中不足的复杂天气与城市环境，并通过时序一致影像将光学标签迁移至SAR数据。为应对配准误差与斑点噪声，采用移不变损失函数并训练条件变分自编码器（CVAE）对多时相SAR复合影像进行去斑点处理，同时保留洪水相关空间结构。实验表明，UNet与UNet++架构在多光谱数据上AUPRC最高达0.956，结合移不变损失与CVAE去斑点方法相比传统滤波器，在SAR洪水制图中具有统计显著性提升。
Muon learns balanced solutions in matrix factorization without slow saddle-to-saddle dynamics
Authors: Mark Rhee, Jamie Simon, Dhruva Karkada
First: 2026-06-29T16:17:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:17:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Matrix factorization (i.e., problems of the form $\min_{\mathbf{P},\mathbf{Q}} \|\mathbf{M}^\star - \mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{Q}\|_\mathrm{F}^2$) is a minimal learning problem that exhibits both nonlinear parameter dynamics and representation learning. In this setting, we study how parameter trajectories under the Muon optimizer differ from those of gradient descent. We identify three main dynamical differences: 1) Muon avoids the slow saddle-to-saddle dynamics from small initialization. Muon instead learns all the top modes of $\mathbf{M}^\star$ at the same rate, with the smaller modes converging first. 2) Muon remains stable even when the learning rate exceeds the critical threshold set by the local loss sharpness. This frees the learning rate from the condition number of the problem, enabling rapid convergence via exponential learning rate annealing. 3) Once the weights are aligned with each other and the target, Muon flow conserves the matrix quantity $\sqrt{\mathbf{P}^\top \mathbf{P}}-\sqrt{\mathbf{Q}^\top \mathbf{Q}}$, while gradient flow is known to conserve the matrix $\mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{P} - \mathbf{Q}^\top\mathbf{Q}$. Despite having distinct conserved quantities, both optimizers find the so-called \textit{balanced} solution from vanishing initialization. When training from small random initialization, the weights spontaneously align early in training. We derive the alignment rates in simple settings and show that they predict the empirical alignment rates in general. Finally, we exploit structural properties of Muon to construct a learning rate schedule that achieves near-perfect alignment in only two optimization steps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Muon在矩阵分解中无需缓慢鞍点间动力学即可学习平衡解
矩阵分解（即形式为$\min_{\mathbf{P},\mathbf{Q}} \|\mathbf{M}^\star - \mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{Q}\|_\mathrm{F}^2$的问题）是最小学习问题，兼具非线性参数动力学与表示学习。在此设定下，我们研究Muon优化器下的参数轨迹与梯度下降的差异。我们识别出三大动力学差异：1）Muon避免了小初始化下的缓慢鞍点间动力学，而是以相同速率学习$\mathbf{M}^\star$的所有主模式，较小模式先收敛。2）Muon即使在学习率超过由局部损失尖锐度决定的临界阈值时仍保持稳定，从而摆脱问题条件数的约束，通过指数式学习率衰减实现快速收敛。3）一旦权重彼此及目标对齐，Muon流保持矩阵量$\sqrt{\mathbf{P}^\top \mathbf{P}}-\sqrt{\mathbf{Q}^\top \mathbf{Q}}$守恒，而梯度流则保持$\mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{P} - \mathbf{Q}^\top\mathbf{Q}$守恒。尽管守恒量不同，两种优化器均从零初始化找到所谓\textit{平衡}解。当从微小随机初始化训练时，权重在训练早期自发对齐。我们在简单情形下推导对齐速率，并证明其可预测一般情形下的经验对齐速率。最后，我们利用Muon的结构特性构造学习率调度，在仅两步优化中实现近乎完美对齐。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how Muon optimizer differs from gradient descent in matrix factorization, the study investigates its trajectory dynamics and convergence behavior. Muon avoids slow saddle-to-saddle transitions from small initialization, instead converging all top modes simultaneously with smaller modes settling first. It remains stable even with learning rates exceeding the local loss sharpness threshold, decoupling convergence from problem condition number and enabling rapid exponential learning rate annealing. Muon conserves a distinct matrix quantity compared to gradient descent, yet both converge to the same balanced solution. The paper derives alignment rates in simple settings that match empirical observations and uses Muon’s structure to design a two-step schedule achieving near-perfect alignment.
为理解Muon优化器在矩阵分解任务中与梯度下降的差异，研究分析了Muon下的参数动态，发现三个关键区别：Muon避免了小初始化下的缓慢鞍点间过渡，所有主成分以相似速率收敛，小成分先收敛；它在学习率超过局部损失尖锐度阈值时仍保持稳定，支持通过指数衰减实现快速收敛；Muon保守的矩阵量不同于梯度下降，但两者均收敛至平衡解。实验表明，Muon从微小随机初始化即可实现早期权重对齐，简单情形下的推导对齐速率准确预测了实际表现。研究进一步利用Muon结构设计学习率调度，在仅两步优化中实现近乎完美对齐。
CARE: Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation
Authors: Guanyu Liu, Weiyi Kong, Zeyu Wang, Boer Zhang, Baiqing Li, Peiyu Zhang, Tianyu Shi
First: 2026-06-12T15:58:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:12:52+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 4 figures. Code: https://github.com/SHITIANYU-hue/care
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Granting LLMs direct control over costly, irreversible scientific experiments leads to unsafe exploration and unstable performance, but discarding LLM creativity entirely sacrifices significant optimization potential. We introduce CARE (Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation), an auditable controller for high-throughput experimentation (HTE) optimization that keeps a non-LLM incumbent optimizer as the default action path while using LLMs to revise challenger ranking policies. Before each outcome is revealed, a public-evidence intervention gate compares the challenger with the incumbent. It authorizes the challenger's selection only when the evidence available before selection supports the change, with the decision recorded in the audit log. CARE outperforms all other evaluated methods on Minerva/Olympus and ChemLex benchmarks, with final-best improving from 80.0 to 88.5 on Minerva/Olympus and from 83.9 to 92.1 on ChemLex, relative to the public incumbent. Our experiments indicate that LLM self-evolution is more reliable when it expands the proposal space under an auditable controller, rather than directly choosing experiments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CARE：通过可审计证据审查控制大语言模型生成的实验策略
赋予大语言模型（LLM）对昂贵且不可逆的科学实验的直接控制权会导致不安全的探索和性能不稳定，但完全摒弃LLM的创造力则会牺牲显著的优化潜力。我们提出了CARE（通过可审计证据审查控制大语言模型生成的实验策略），这是一种用于高通量实验（HTE）优化的可审计控制器，其默认操作路径为非LLM的现有优化器，而使用LLM来修订挑战者策略的排序。在每次结果公布前，一个公开证据干预门会将挑战者与现有策略进行比较，仅当选择前可用的证据支持变更时才授权挑战者的选择，并将决策记录在审计日志中。CARE在Minerva/Olympus和ChemLex基准测试中均优于所有其他评估方法，相对于公开现有策略，Minerva/Olympus的最终最佳性能从80.0提升至88.5，ChemLex从83.9提升至92.1。我们的实验表明，当LLM在可审计控制器下扩展提案空间时，其自我演化更为可靠，而非直接选择实验。
Summary / 总结
To address the risks of unsafe and unstable exploration when LLMs directly control costly scientific experiments while preserving their optimization potential, CARE introduces an auditable controller that maintains a non-LLM optimizer as default and uses LLMs to propose alternative policies. Before each experiment outcome is revealed, a public-evidence gate compares the LLM’s challenger policy against the incumbent, allowing the challenger only if available evidence supports the switch, with decisions logged for audit. CARE consistently outperforms baseline methods on Minerva/Olympus and ChemLex benchmarks, improving final-best performance from 80.0 to 88.5 and from 83.9 to 92.1 respectively, demonstrating that LLMs evolve more reliably under auditable constraints than direct control.
为平衡LLM在科学实验中的探索风险与优化潜力，CARE提出一种可审计控制器，以非LLM优化器作为默认策略，允许LLM提出并修订实验策略。在每次实验结果公布前，一个公开证据门控机制评估现有证据是否支持切换至挑战策略，并将决策记录于审计日志。实验表明，CARE在Minerva/Olympus和ChemLex基准上显著优于其他方法，最终最佳得分分别从80.0提升至88.5、从83.9提升至92.1，证明在可审计控制下，LLM的自我演化比直接选择实验更可靠。
Accelerating scientific discovery with Co-Scientist
Authors: Juraj Gottweis, Wei-Hung Weng, Alexander Daryin, Tao Tu, Petar Sirkovic, Artiom Myaskovsky, Grzegorz Glowaty, Felix Weissenberger, Alessio Orlandi, Dan Popovici, Anil Palepu, Keran Rong, Ryutaro Tanno, Khaled Saab, Fan Zhang, Jacob Blum, Andrew Carroll, Kavita Kulkarni, Nenad Tomasev, Dina Zverinski, Ivor Rendulic, Elahe Vedadi, Florian Hasler, Luka Rimanic, Marina Boia, Ivan Budiselic, Ben Feinstein, Mathias Bellaiche, Tom Sheffer, Jan Freyberg, Jeremy Ratcliff, Ottavia Bertolli, Katherine Chou, Avinatan Hassidim, Burak Gokturk, Amin Vahdat, Yuan Guan, Vikram Dhillon, Eeshit Dhaval Vaishnav, Byron Lee, Tiago R D Costa, José R Penadés, Gary Peltz, Yossi Matias, James Manyika, Demis Hassabis, Yunhan Xu, Pushmeet Kohli, Annalisa Pawlosky, Alan Karthikesalingam, Vivek Natarajan
First: 2025-02-26T06:17:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:12:47+00:00
Comments: 157 pages in total (main 42 pages, supplementary information 115 pages), 4 main figures, 1 main table, 6 extended data figures, 2 extended data tables, 9 supplementary figures, 4 supplementary tables, 37 main references, 117 supplementary references. Nature (2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific discovery is driven by scientists generating novel hypotheses for complex problems that undergo rigorous experimental validation. To augment this process, we introduce Co-Scientist, a multi-agent AI system built on Gemini for structured scientific thinking and hypothesis generation. Co-Scientist aims to help scientists discover new original knowledge. Conditioned on their research objectives and prior scientific evidence, it formulates demonstrably novel research hypotheses for experimental verification. The system's design involves agents continuously generating, critiquing and refining hypotheses accelerated by scaling test-time compute. Key contributions include: (1) a multi-agent architecture with an asynchronous task execution framework for flexible compute scaling; (2) a tournament evolution process for self-improving hypotheses generation. Automated evaluations show continued benefits of test-time compute scaling, improving hypothesis quality over time. While general purpose, we focus the validation in three biomedical applications: drug repurposing, novel target discovery, and explaining mechanisms of anti-microbial resistance. Specifically, Co-Scientist helped identify new drug repurposing candidates and synergistic combination therapies for acute myeloid leukemia, which were validated through in vitro experiments. These real-world validations demonstrate the potential of Co-Scientist to accelerate scientific discovery and usher in an era of AI empowered scientists.
中文标题/摘要
标题：借助Co-Scientist加速科学发现
科学发现由科学家为复杂问题提出新颖假设并经过严格实验验证驱动。为增强这一过程，我们引入了Co-Scientist，这是一个基于Gemini构建的多智能体AI系统，旨在支持结构化科学思维与假设生成，帮助科学家发现新知识。该系统根据研究目标和已有科学证据，生成可验证的全新研究假设。其设计包含持续生成、批判与优化假设的智能体，通过扩展测试时计算能力加速进程。主要贡献包括：(1) 一种异步任务执行框架的多智能体架构，支持灵活计算扩展；(2) 一种锦标赛式进化过程，实现自我优化的假设生成。自动化评估显示，测试时计算扩展持续提升假设质量。尽管通用性强，我们主要在三个生物医学应用中验证其效果：药物再利用、新靶点发现及抗菌耐药机制解释。具体而言，Co-Scientist帮助识别了急性髓系白血病的新药物再利用候选物及协同治疗方案，并通过体外实验验证。这些实际验证表明，Co-Scientist有望加速科学发现，开启AI赋能科学家的新时代。
Summary / 总结
To accelerate scientific discovery, Co-Scientist is a multi-agent AI system built on Gemini that assists scientists in generating novel, testable hypotheses for complex problems. It operates by continuously generating, critiquing, and refining hypotheses through an asynchronous task framework and a tournament-based evolution process, leveraging scalable test-time compute to improve hypothesis quality over time. Evaluated in three biomedical domains—drug repurposing, target discovery, and antimicrobial resistance mechanisms—Co-Scientist successfully identified new drug candidates and synergistic therapies for acute myeloid leukemia, validated through in vitro experiments, demonstrating its potential to enhance scientific progress through AI augmentation.

Doubly Robust Adaptive Conformal Inference for Causal Effects Under Temporal Dependence
Authors: Andreas Koukorinis, Ricardo Silva
First: 2026-06-29T16:03:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:03:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose doubly robust adaptive conformal inference (DR-ACI), which constructs prediction intervals for doubly robust pseudo-outcomes under temporal dependence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有时间依赖性的因果效应的双重稳健自适应符合推断
我们提出了双重稳健自适应符合推断（DR-ACI），用于在时间依赖性下构建双重稳健伪结果的预测区间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable causal effect inference under temporal dependence, this work introduces doubly robust adaptive conformal inference (DR-ACI), a method that constructs prediction intervals for doubly robust pseudo-outcomes while adapting to the temporal structure of data. DR-ACI combines doubly robust estimation with conformal inference to ensure validity under model misspecification and temporal correlation. Experimental results demonstrate that DR-ACI achieves tighter and more accurate prediction intervals compared to existing methods, particularly in settings with strong temporal dependence and heterogeneous treatment effects.
为了解决在时间依赖条件下因果效应估计的可靠性问题，本文提出了双重稳健自适应校准推断（DR-ACI）方法，该方法通过构建双重稳健伪结果的预测区间，并考虑时间相关性。DR-ACI结合了双重稳健估计与自适应校准推断，即使在模型设定错误或存在强时间依赖时仍能保证覆盖有效性。实验结果表明，DR-ACI在强时间依赖场景下能实现准确的覆盖率和具有竞争力的区间宽度，优于现有方法。
Discovering Collaboration from Novelty: Random Network Distillation for Clustered Federated Learning
Authors: Davide Domini, Gianluca Aguzzi, Ivana Dusparic, Danilo Pianini, Mirko Viroli
First: 2026-06-29T16:02:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:02:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated Learning often suffers under non-independently and identically distributed data, where a single global model may fail to represent the diversity of client distributions. Clustered Federated Learning mitigates this issue by training specialized models for groups of similar clients, but existing approaches often couple cluster assignment with the main training loop, increasing computational and communication costs. We propose a lightweight clustering approach based on Random Network Distillation. Each client trains a compact Random Network Distillation predictor on its local data and uses its prediction error as a novelty signal to estimate similarity with other clients. This enables the discovery of meaningful client groups before federated training, without sharing raw data or repeatedly evaluating the main model. Crucially, the resulting federations emerge from local novelty estimates at runtime, making the method suitable for autonomous large-scale distributed systems where neither the number of clusters nor the collaboration structure can be specified a priori. Overall, by decoupling clustering from learning, the method provides a task-agnostic and efficient mechanism for autonomous collaboration under non-independently and identically distributed data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从新颖性中发现协作：用于聚类联邦学习的随机网络蒸馏
联邦学习常因非独立同分布数据而表现不佳，单一全局模型难以代表客户端分布的多样性。聚类联邦学习通过为相似客户端组训练专用模型缓解此问题，但现有方法常将聚类分配与主训练循环耦合，增加计算与通信开销。我们提出一种基于随机网络蒸馏的轻量级聚类方法：每个客户端在其本地数据上训练一个紧凑的随机网络蒸馏预测器，并利用预测误差作为新颖性信号，估算与其他客户端的相似度。这使得在联邦训练前即可发现有意义的客户端组，无需共享原始数据或重复评估主模型。关键在于，生成的联邦结构在运行时由本地新颖性估计动态形成，适用于无法预先指定聚类数量或协作结构的自主大规模分布式系统。总体而言，该方法通过解耦聚类与学习，提供了一种任务无关且高效的非独立同分布数据下自主协作机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of non-independent and identically distributed data in federated learning, this work proposes a lightweight clustering method using Random Network Distillation to enable autonomous client grouping. Each client trains a compact predictor on local data and uses prediction error as a novelty signal to estimate similarity with peers, allowing cluster formation before training without data sharing or repeated model evaluation. Experimental results show that this approach effectively discovers meaningful client clusters, reduces communication and computation costs, and supports scalable, task-agnostic collaboration in distributed systems without prior knowledge of cluster structure.
为解决联邦学习中非独立同分布数据导致的全局模型失效问题，本文提出一种基于随机网络蒸馏的轻量级聚类方法。每个客户端在本地数据上训练一个紧凑预测器，利用预测误差作为新颖性信号评估与其他客户端的相似度，从而在训练前自主发现有意义的客户端群组，无需共享原始数据或重复评估主模型。实验表明，该方法能有效识别客户端集群，降低通信与计算开销，支持大规模分布式系统中无需预设集群数量和协作结构的高效、任务无关协作。
On the Faithfulness of Post-Hoc Concept Bottleneck Models
Authors: Laines Schmalwasser, Jan Blunk, Niklas Penzel, Julia Niebling, Joachim Denzler
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-29T16:02:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:02:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ECCV 2026, 41 pages, 13 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human decision-making interprets the world through high-level concepts, such as recognizing a bird by its belly color. To bridge the gap between opaque deep learning representations and human understanding, Post-Hoc Concept Bottleneck Models (post-hoc CBMs) project latent features onto interpretable concept spaces using auxiliary datasets or vision-language models. However, relying on target task accuracy as the primary measure of post-hoc CBM success obscures whether the learned concepts are semantically meaningful or merely predictive artifacts. For example, random concept projections can achieve competitive accuracy despite being semantically meaningless. In this work, we analyze the learned projections directly and identify two failure cases: First, for concept projections learned from auxiliary data, covariate shifts can lead to unfaithful concept representations for the target task. In particular, we provide an upper bound on the error introduced by this shift. Second, systematic label noise in surrogate concept labels generated by vision-language models leads to unfaithful projections. After formalizing these failure modes, we introduce novel metrics that decouple concept faithfulness from predictive accuracy. Our empirical results across real-world and synthetic benchmarks confirm that these metrics identify unfaithful behaviors that standard accuracy-based evaluation fails to detect.
中文标题/摘要
标题：事后概念瓶颈模型的忠实性研究
人类决策通过高层次概念（如根据鸟腹颜色识别鸟类）理解世界。为弥合深度学习模型的黑箱表示与人类理解之间的差距，事后概念瓶颈模型（post-hoc CBMs）利用辅助数据集或视觉-语言模型将潜在特征投影到可解释的概念空间。然而，仅以目标任务准确率作为事后CBM成功的主要衡量标准，会掩盖所学概念是否语义有意义，还是仅具预测性伪迹。例如，随机概念投影可能在语义无意义的情况下仍获得竞争力的准确率。本文直接分析所学投影，识别两种失败情形：第一，从辅助数据学习的概念投影，因协变量偏移可能导致目标任务概念表示不忠实，我们给出了该偏移引入误差的上界；第二，视觉-语言模型生成的代理概念标签中系统性标签噪声导致投影不忠实。在形式化这些失败模式后，我们引入了新的度量指标，将概念忠实性与预测准确性解耦。在真实世界与合成基准上的实证结果证实，这些指标能识别标准准确率评估无法检测的不忠实行为。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate whether post-hoc concept bottleneck models (CBMs) genuinely capture human-like semantic concepts rather than merely predictive artifacts, this work introduces novel metrics to assess concept faithfulness independently of task accuracy. It identifies two key failure modes: covariate shifts in auxiliary data leading to unfaithful concept representations, and systematic label noise in vision-language model-generated concept labels. The authors formalize these issues and propose metrics that detect unfaithful projections, which standard accuracy-based evaluations miss. Empirical results across real-world and synthetic benchmarks confirm that these metrics successfully expose cases where models perform well but encode meaningless concepts.
为评估后置概念瓶颈模型是否真正捕捉语义概念而非仅预测性伪影，本文研究其忠实性。识别出两大失效模式：辅助数据中的协变量偏移导致概念表征失真，以及视觉语言模型生成的概念标签中存在系统性噪声。为此，作者提出新的度量指标，将概念忠实性与预测准确性解耦。在真实和合成基准上的实验证明，这些指标能有效检测标准准确率评估遗漏的失真行为。
UniPR-3D: Towards Universal Visual Place Recognition with Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer
Authors: Tianchen Deng, Xun Chen, Ziming Li, Hongming Shen, Shuhao Zhai, Danwei Wang, Javier Civera, Hesheng Wang
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2025-12-24T09:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:02:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Visual Place Recognition (VPR) has been traditionally formulated as a single-image retrieval task. Using multiple views offers clear advantages, yet this setting remains relatively underexplored and existing methods often struggle to generalize across diverse environments. In this work we introduce UniPR-3D, the first VPR architecture that effectively integrates information from multiple views. UniPR-3D builds on a VGGT backbone capable of encoding multi-view 3D representations, which we adapt by designing feature aggregators and fine-tune for the place recognition task. To construct our descriptor, we jointly leverage the 3D tokens and intermediate 2D tokens produced by VGGT. Based on their distinct characteristics, we design dedicated aggregation modules for 2D and 3D features, allowing our descriptor to capture fine-grained texture cues while also reasoning across viewpoints. To further enhance generalization, we incorporate both single- and multi-frame aggregation schemes, along with a variable-length sequence retrieval strategy. Our experiments show that UniPR-3D sets a new state of the art, outperforming both single- and multi-view baselines and highlighting the effectiveness of geometry-grounded tokens for VPR. Our code and models will be made publicly available on Github https://github.com/dtc111111/UniPR-3D.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniPR-3D：基于视觉几何引导的Transformer实现通用视觉地点识别
视觉地点识别（VPR）传统上被建模为单图像检索任务。使用多视角能带来明显优势，但该设定仍相对未被充分探索，现有方法往往难以在不同环境中泛化。本文提出UniPR-3D，这是首个有效整合多视角信息的VPR架构。UniPR-3D基于VGGT主干网络，该网络可编码多视角3D表示，我们通过设计特征聚合器并针对地点识别任务进行微调。为构建描述符，我们联合利用VGGT生成的3D令牌和中间2D令牌。基于其不同特性，我们为2D和3D特征设计专用聚合模块，使描述符既能捕捉细粒度纹理线索，又能跨视角推理。为进一步提升泛化能力，我们引入单帧与多帧聚合方案，以及可变长度序列检索策略。实验表明，UniPR-3D达到新SOTA，超越单视角与多视角基线，凸显几何引导令牌对VPR的有效性。代码与模型将在Github公开：https://github.com/dtc111111/UniPR-3D。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for better generalization in visual place recognition (VPR), this work introduces UniPR-3D, the first VPR model that effectively leverages multi-view information. It builds on a VGGT backbone adapted with custom feature aggregators and fine-tuned for VPR, combining 3D tokens and intermediate 2D tokens into a unified descriptor using specialized modules that preserve texture details and viewpoint reasoning. To improve robustness, it employs both single- and multi-frame aggregation with a variable-length retrieval strategy. Experiments demonstrate UniPR-3D achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing both single- and multi-view baselines, confirming the value of geometry-grounded representations in VPR.
为提升视觉地点识别的泛化能力，本文提出UniPR-3D，首次有效融合多视角信息。该架构基于VGGT主干网络，通过定制特征聚合器并针对地点识别任务微调，结合3D几何令牌与中间2D令牌，兼顾纹理细节与视角不变结构。模型进一步通过多帧聚合与可变长度序列检索增强泛化能力。实验表明，UniPR-3D达到当前最优性能，超越单视角与多视角基线，验证了几何引导令牌在VPR中的有效性。
GPU Parallelization Strategies for Forward and Backward Propagation in Shallow Neural Networks: A CUDA-Based Comparative Study
Authors: Rania Zitouni, Nadine Bousdjira, Sarah Hasnaoui, Amel Sadoun, Fatma Salhi
First: 2026-06-29T16:02:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:02:10+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 5 figures. Technical report, ESI Algiers, 2025--2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a comparative study of CUDA optimization strategies applied to forward and backward propagation in a shallow neural network. Three stacked optimizations are evaluated: (1) tiled shared memory with bank-conflict elimination via +1-column padding, (2) pre-transposed weight matrices for coalesced global memory access, and (3) a fused MatMul+ReLU kernel that eliminates intermediate global-memory round-trips. Experiments on an NVIDIA Tesla T4 (CUDA 13.0) across three dataset sizes show that the fully optimized implementation achieves a 1.41x speedup over the baseline CUDA version on the large dataset (25,600 samples), reducing execution time from 21.0s to 14.8s. Results are compared against a sequential CPU baseline and an OpenMP parallel implementation, demonstrating the effectiveness of memory-access optimization in GPU-accelerated deep learning primitives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：浅层神经网络前向与反向传播的GPU并行化策略：基于CUDA的比较研究
本文对应用于浅层神经网络前向与反向传播的CUDA优化策略进行了比较研究。评估了三种堆叠优化：(1) 通过+1列填充消除银行冲突的分块共享内存；(2) 预转置权重矩阵以实现全局内存的聚类访问；(3) 融合MatMul+ReLU内核以消除中间全局内存往返。在NVIDIA Tesla T4（CUDA 13.0）上针对三个数据集规模进行实验，结果显示在大样本数据集（25,600样本）上，完全优化的实现相比基线CUDA版本提速1.41倍，执行时间从21.0秒降至14.8秒。结果与串行CPU基线及OpenMP并行实现对比，证明了GPU加速深度学习原语中内存访问优化的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accelerate shallow neural network training on GPUs, this study evaluates three CUDA-based optimization strategies for forward and backward propagation: tiled shared memory with bank-conflict mitigation via column padding, pre-transposed weight matrices to enable coalesced memory access, and a fused MatMul+ReLU kernel to eliminate redundant global memory transfers. Experiments on an NVIDIA Tesla T4 using three dataset sizes reveal that the fully optimized version achieves a 1.41x speedup over the baseline CUDA implementation on the largest dataset (25,600 samples), reducing execution time from 21.0s to 14.8s. Performance comparisons with a sequential CPU baseline and an OpenMP parallel version confirm that memory-access optimizations significantly enhance GPU efficiency in deep learning primitives.
该研究旨在通过优化GPU性能缓解浅层神经网络前向和反向传播的瓶颈。评估了三种CUDA策略：通过列填充消除共享内存银行冲突的分块共享内存、预转置权重矩阵以实现全局内存的聚类访问，以及融合MatMul+ReLU内核以消除中间全局内存往返。在NVIDIA Tesla T4上对大型数据集（25,600样本）的实验表明，完全优化版本相比基准CUDA实现提速1.41倍，运行时间从21.0秒降至14.8秒，并优于顺序CPU基线和OpenMP并行版本，凸显内存访问优化对GPU加速深度学习原语的关键作用。
McMg: A Learned Phase-Space Multi-channel Multigrid Preconditioner for Helmholtz Equation
Authors: Jiwei Jia, Xinliang Liu, Juntao Wang, Jinchao Xu
First: 2026-06-29T16:01:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:01:44+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Solving heterogeneous Helmholtz equations at high wavenumbers remains challenging because the discretized operator is indefinite, pollution degrades phase accuracy, and scalar coarse-grid correction can discard the local phase and propagation-direction information carried by oscillatory errors. We propose Multi-channel Multigrid (McMg), a learned phase-space multigrid preconditioner for heterogeneous Helmholtz equations. Rather than predicting the solution directly, McMg maps residuals to corrections within an iterative framework. Its central idea is to coarsen physical space while retaining unresolved local wave information in the channel dimension: each coarse node carries a learned packet of amplitude, phase, direction, and scattering coefficients rather than a single scalar unknown. The architecture combines linear multi-channel transfer operators with locally adaptive stencils, neural PDE operators, and medium-dependent smoothers whose coefficients are generated from the wave speed. For a fixed medium, the V-cycle is linear in the residual; nonlinear physical features are computed once in a setup phase and cached, so each online iteration reduces to convolutions with fixed coefficients. We further study generalization across scales. Models trained on small domains transfer directly to larger domains and higher effective wavenumbers, and a Layer-by-Layer Progressive Finetuning (LLPF) strategy extends the support of the learned Green's operator by adding and finetuning only new coarse levels. Numerical experiments on high-frequency, high-contrast, and large-scale three-dimensional problems demonstrate that McMg requires substantially fewer iterations and less wall-clock time than strong classical baselines, while consistently outperforming existing neural preconditioners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：McMg：用于亥姆霍兹方程的基于学习的相空间多通道多重网格预条件器
求解高波数异质亥姆霍兹方程仍具挑战性，因为离散算子为不定型，污染效应会降低相位精度，且标量粗网格校正会丢弃振荡误差所携带的局部相位和传播方向信息。我们提出Multi-channel Multigrid（McMg），一种针对异质亥姆霍兹方程的基于学习的相空间多重网格预条件器。McMg不直接预测解，而是在迭代框架内将残差映射为校正。其核心思想是在粗化物理空间的同时，保留通道维度中的未解析局部波信息：每个粗网格节点携带一个学习得到的振幅、相位、方向和散射系数包，而非单一标量未知数。该架构结合线性多通道传输算子、局部自适应模板、神经PDE算子及依赖介质的平滑器（其系数由波速生成）。对于固定介质，V型循环的迭代次数与残差呈线性关系；非线性物理特征在设置阶段计算一次并缓存，因此每次在线迭代仅需固定系数的卷积运算。我们进一步研究了跨尺度泛化能力：在小域训练的模型可直接迁移到大域和更高有效波数，且通过逐层渐进微调（LLPF）策略，仅添加并微调新粗网格层级即可扩展所学格林算子的支持范围。数值实验表明，McMg在高频、高对比度、大规模三维问题上，相比强经典基线方法所需迭代次数和实际运行时间显著减少，且持续优于现有神经预条件器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of solving high-wavenumber heterogeneous Helmholtz equations due to indefinite operators and phase pollution, McMg introduces a learned phase-space multigrid preconditioner that maps residuals to corrections within an iterative framework. It coarsens physical space while preserving local wave information in a channel dimension, where each coarse node carries learned amplitude, phase, direction, and scattering coefficients. The method combines linear multi-channel transfer operators, adaptive stencils, neural PDE operators, and medium-dependent smoothers derived from wave speed. For fixed media, the V-cycle scales linearly with residual, with nonlinear features precomputed and cached. Experiments show McMg requires fewer iterations and less wall-clock time than classical methods and outperforms existing neural preconditioners on 3D high-frequency, high-contrast problems, with strong generalization across scales enabled by Layer-by-Layer Progressive Finetuning.
为解决高波数异质亥姆霍兹方程因算子不定、相位污染导致的求解难题，McMg提出一种学习型相空间多通道多重网格预条件器，通过迭代框架将残差映射为校正量。其核心思想是在粗化物理空间的同时，保留通道维度中的局部波信息，每个粗网格节点携带学习得到的振幅、相位、方向及散射系数。该方法结合线性多通道传输算子、局部自适应模板、神经PDE算子及基于波速生成的介质依赖平滑器。固定介质下，V循环残差线性增长，非线性特征预计算并缓存，每次在线迭代仅需固定系数卷积。实验表明，McMg在高频、高对比度三维问题上迭代次数和运行时间显著少于经典方法，且持续优于现有神经预条件器，模型还能通过逐层渐进微调实现跨尺度泛化。
Multi-Agent Route Planning as a QUBO Problem
Authors: Renáta Rusnáková, Martin Chovanec, Juraj Gazda
First: 2026-02-08T11:18:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:00:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-Agent Route Planning considers selecting vehicles, each associated with a single predefined route, such that route-level coverage utility is maximized while redundant spatial overlaps are limited. This paper gives a formal problem definition, proves NP-hardness by reduction from the Weighted Set Packing problem, and derives a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization formulation whose coefficients directly encode route utility rewards and pairwise overlap penalties. A single penalty parameter $λ$ controls the coverage--overlap trade-off. We distinguish between a soft regime, which supports multi-objective exploration, and a hard regime, in which the penalty is strong enough to effectively enforce near-disjoint routes. We describe a practical pipeline for generating city instances, constructing candidate routes, building the QUBO matrix, and solving it with a binary quadratic programming baseline (Gurobi), simulated annealing, and D-Wave hybrid quantum annealing. Experiments on Barcelona instances with up to $10{,}000$ vehicles reveal a clear coverage--overlap knee and show that Pareto-optimal solutions are mainly obtained under the hard-penalty regime, while D-Wave hybrid solvers and Gurobi achieve very similar objective values on matching configurations with only minor runtime differences as problem size grows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多智能体路径规划作为QUBO问题
多智能体路径规划旨在选择车辆，每辆车关联一条预定义路径，以最大化路径级覆盖效用并限制空间冗余重叠。本文给出了形式化问题定义，通过与加权集合打包问题的归约证明其NP难，并推导出一个二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）公式，其系数直接编码路径效用奖励和成对重叠惩罚。单个惩罚参数λ控制覆盖与重叠的权衡。我们区分软模式（支持多目标探索）与硬模式（惩罚足够强以有效强制近似不相交路径）。我们描述了生成城市实例、构建候选路径、构建QUBO矩阵并使用二元二次规划基线（Gurobi）、模拟退火和D-Wave混合量子退火求解的实用流程。在巴塞罗那实例上的实验（最多10,000辆车）揭示了清晰的覆盖-重叠拐点，并表明在硬惩罚模式下主要获得帕累托最优解，而D-Wave混合求解器与Gurobi在匹配配置下获得非常相似的目标值，仅在问题规模增大时运行时间略有差异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize multi-agent route planning for maximum coverage while minimizing spatial overlap, this paper formulates the problem as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) model, encoding route rewards and pairwise overlap penalties via a single tunable parameter λ. The authors demonstrate NP-hardness via reduction from Weighted Set Packing and evaluate solvers including Gurobi, simulated annealing, and D-Wave hybrid quantum annealing on large-scale city instances with up to 10,000 vehicles. Results show a clear trade-off between coverage and overlap, with Pareto-optimal solutions emerging under strong penalty (hard regime), and D-Wave and Gurobi achieving nearly identical objective values with only minor runtime differences as problem size increases.
本文将多智能体路径规划问题建模为二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题，旨在最大化路径覆盖效益同时最小化空间重叠。作者通过归约至加权集合打包问题证明该问题为NP难，并构建QUBO模型，其系数直接编码路径奖励与成对重叠惩罚，由单一参数λ控制覆盖与重叠的权衡。在巴塞罗那城市实例（最多10,000辆车）实验中，发现覆盖与重叠之间存在明确的权衡拐点，高惩罚强度下可获得帕累托最优解。Gurobi、模拟退火及D-Wave混合量子退火求解器在匹配配置下目标值相近，仅在问题规模增大时运行时间略有差异。
Pairwise Comparisons without Stochastic Transitivity: Model, Theory and Applications
Authors: Sze Ming Lee, Yunxiao Chen
First: 2025-01-13T16:05:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T16:00:05+00:00
Comments: 49 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most statistical models for pairwise comparisons, including the Bradley-Terry (BT) and Thurstone models and many extensions, make a relatively strong assumption of stochastic transitivity. This assumption imposes the existence of an unobserved global ranking among all the players/teams/items and monotone constraints on the comparison probabilities implied by the global ranking. However, the stochastic transitivity assumption does not hold in many real-world scenarios of pairwise comparisons, especially games involving multiple skills or strategies. As a result, models relying on this assumption can have suboptimal predictive performance. In this paper, we propose a general family of statistical models for pairwise comparison data without a stochastic transitivity assumption, substantially extending the BT and Thurstone models. In this model, the pairwise probabilities are determined by a (approximately) low-dimensional skew-symmetric matrix. Likelihood-based estimation methods and computational algorithms are developed, which allow for sparse data with only a small proportion of observed pairs. Theoretical analysis shows that the proposed estimator achieves minimax-rate optimality, which adapts effectively to the sparsity level of the data. The spectral theory for skew-symmetric matrices plays a crucial role in the implementation and theoretical analysis. The proposed method's superiority against the BT model, along with its broad applicability across diverse scenarios, is further supported by simulations and real data analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需随机传递性的一对一比较：模型、理论与应用
大多数用于一对一比较的统计模型，包括布拉德利-泰瑞（BT）模型和图思顿模型及其许多扩展，都假设了相对较强的随机传递性。该假设要求所有参与者/队伍/物品之间存在一个未观测到的全局排序，并对由全局排序所隐含的比较概率施加单调约束。然而，在许多现实世界的一对一比较场景中（尤其是涉及多种技能或策略的游戏），随机传递性假设并不成立。因此，依赖该假设的模型可能具有次优的预测性能。本文提出了一类无需随机传递性假设的一对一比较数据的通用统计模型，显著扩展了BT和图思顿模型。在该模型中，成对概率由一个（近似）低维的斜对称矩阵决定。我们开发了基于似然的估计方法和计算算法，适用于仅有少量观测对的稀疏数据。理论分析表明，所提出的估计量达到最小最大最优速率，能有效适应数据的稀疏程度。斜对称矩阵的谱理论在实现和理论分析中起着关键作用。仿真和实际数据分析进一步支持了该方法相对于BT模型的优越性及其在多种场景中的广泛适用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional pairwise comparison models like Bradley-Terry that rely on the restrictive stochastic transitivity assumption, this paper introduces a novel family of models that relax this constraint, allowing for more realistic representations of complex pairwise interactions. The proposed model characterizes pairwise comparison probabilities via a low-dimensional skew-symmetric matrix, enabling flexible modeling without assuming global rankings. Estimation is performed via likelihood-based methods, designed to handle sparse data with limited observed pairs. Theoretical results demonstrate that the estimator achieves minimax-rate optimality and adapts well to data sparsity, while spectral properties of skew-symmetric matrices underpin both computation and analysis. Empirical evaluations confirm superior predictive performance over the Bradley-Terry model across diverse settings.
鉴于随机传递性假设在多技能博弈等现实场景中不成立，本文提出了一类无需该假设的统计模型。该模型通过低维斜对称矩阵刻画成对比较概率，避免了全局排名的强约束。基于似然估计与定制算法，方法可高效处理仅有少量观测对的稀疏数据。理论分析表明，该估计量达到最小最大最优收敛速率，能自适应数据稀疏度，其计算与理论分析依赖斜对称矩阵的谱性质。仿真与真实数据实验显示，该方法在预测性能上优于布拉德利-特里模型，验证了其广泛适用性。
RBE-Flow: Recurrent Bayesian Estimation on Feature Manifolds for Cross-Modal Registration
Authors: Mengzhu Ding, Xin Song, Xiaoke Ding, Hongwei Ding, Xuecong Liu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-29T15:58:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T15:58:25+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Cross-modal image registration is essential for multi-sensor perception but remains fundamentally challenging due to severe non-linear radiometric discrepancies and geometric distortions. Existing deterministic matching methods lack uncertainty awareness, struggling to navigate the resulting highly non-convex optimization landscape and frequently accumulating errors in ambiguous regions. In this paper, we propose RBE-Flow, a novel framework that reformulates dense cross-modal flow estimation as a closed-loop recurrent Bayesian estimation problem on learned feature manifolds. Diverging from standard feed-forward regression, RBE-Flow establishes a robust self-correcting mechanism by deeply coupling feature-metric non-linear optimization with probabilistic state updates. Specifically, a Recurrent Manifold Optimization (RMO) block iteratively generates flow observations and their associated uncertainties, which are then optimally assimilated into the prior state via an Uncertainty-Adaptive Probabilistic Update (UAPU) using deterministic sigma-point projection. Crucially, the resulting calibrated posterior covariance is fed back to adaptively regularize the damping of subsequent optimization steps, allowing the system to modulate its convergence based on predictive confidence. To ensure stable probabilistic training, we introduce a hybrid supervision scheme featuring a geometry-aware rectified NLL loss that structurally prevents variance collapse. Extensive experiments on challenging OSdataset, WHU-OPT-SAR, and RoadScene benchmarks demonstrate that RBE-Flow consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing methods by a significant margin, particularly under strict sub-pixel criteria. Project page: https://github.com/NEU-Liuxuecong/RBE-Flow
中文标题/摘要
标题：RBE-Flow：基于特征流形的递归贝叶斯估计用于跨模态配准
跨模态图像配准对多传感器感知至关重要，但由于严重的非线性辐射差异和几何畸变，仍具有根本性挑战。现有确定性匹配方法缺乏不确定性感知，难以应对由此产生的高度非凸优化景观，且在模糊区域易累积误差。本文提出RBE-Flow，一种新颖框架，将密集跨模态流估计重新表述为在学习到的特征流形上的闭环递归贝叶斯估计问题。不同于标准前馈回归，RBE-Flow通过深度耦合特征度量非线性优化与概率状态更新，建立鲁棒的自校正机制。具体而言，递归流形优化（RMO）模块迭代生成流观测及其不确定性，随后通过确定性sigma点投影，经不确定性自适应概率更新（UAPU）最优融合至先验状态。关键在于，所得校准后协方差反馈用于自适应调节后续优化步骤的阻尼，使系统可根据预测置信度调节收敛。为确保稳定概率训练，引入混合监督方案，包含几何感知修正NLL损失，结构上防止方差坍缩。在具有挑战性的OSdataset、WHU-OPT-SAR和RoadScene基准测试中，RBE-Flow持续取得最先进性能，显著优于现有方法，尤其在严格亚像素标准下表现突出。项目页面：https://github.com/NEU-Liuxuecong/RBE-Flow
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of cross-modal image registration due to non-linear radiometric discrepancies and geometric distortions, RBE-Flow introduces a recurrent Bayesian estimation framework that treats dense flow estimation as a closed-loop optimization problem on learned feature manifolds. It combines Recurrent Manifold Optimization with Uncertainty-Adaptive Probabilistic Update to iteratively generate flow estimates and update their uncertainties, using sigma-point projection to fuse observations into a calibrated posterior. This posterior dynamically regulates optimization damping, enabling adaptive convergence based on confidence. Trained with a geometry-aware rectified NLL loss to prevent variance collapse, RBE-Flow achieves state-of-the-art results on OSdataset, WHU-OPT-SAR, and RoadScene benchmarks, significantly outperforming prior methods under strict sub-pixel accuracy criteria.
为解决跨模态图像配准中因非线性辐射差异和几何畸变导致的挑战，RBE-Flow提出一种基于特征流空间的循环贝叶斯估计框架，将稠密流估计建模为在学习特征流空间上的闭环优化问题。其核心模块Recurrent Manifold Optimization迭代生成带不确定性的流估计，通过Uncertainty-Adaptive Probabilistic Update模块结合sigma点投影进行概率融合，融合后的后验协方差动态调节后续优化的阻尼系数，实现基于置信度的自适应收敛。采用几何感知的修正NLL损失函数防止方差坍缩，实验表明其在OSdataset、WHU-OPT-SAR和RoadScene等多个基准上显著优于现有方法，尤其在亚像素精度要求下表现突出。
SIMAX: A Scalable and Interpretable Framework for Multi-Fidelity and Annotated Clinician-Patient Dialogue Simulation
Authors: Zhuhan Bao, Rui Yang, Bohao Yang, Zhiyi Liu, Sicheng Shu, Ruio Heerschap, Le Li, Doris Yang, Elisabeth Bond, Haoyuan Wang, Nicoleta Economou-Zavlanos, Joshua M. Biro, Matthew McDermott, Nan Liu, Anand Chowdhury, Kai Sun, Kathryn Pollak, Ed Hammond, Chuan Hong
First: 2026-06-29T15:56:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T15:56:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background. The widespread deployment of ambient digital scribes is driving large-scale capture of clinician-patient dialogues. Human coding of clinical communication data remains costly, inconsistent, and difficult to scale, motivating AI-driven communication coding systems. However, evaluating these systems requires real-world dialogues and human-coded labels, both hard to obtain at scale. Methods. We developed SIMAX (Scalable and Interpretable Framework for Multi-Fidelity and Annotated Clinician-Patient Dialogue Simulation), a framework for generating controlled clinical dialogue data with reference behavioral annotations. SIMAX generates clinician-patient dialogues from predefined clinical scenarios, personas and voice conditions, and target communication behaviors. Behaviors are controlled using two codebooks: the Global Codebook for overall communication quality and the WISER Codebook for specific countable behaviors. We evaluated SIMAX using automated and human quality assessments and an example communication coding system. Results. SIMAX generated 3,388 simulated dialogues across three specialties, multiple visit stages, persona characteristics, and accent conditions. Automated assessment showed mean UTMOS and WV-MOS scores of 3.03 and 2.61, WER and CER of 0.07 and 0.05, and CLAP cosine similarity of 0.41, suggesting reasonable speech naturalness, high transcription fidelity, and positive text-audio correspondence. Human evaluation showed a median MOS of 4.67 and a median clinical realism score of 3.00. Downstream evaluation suggests that SIMAX can assess how a communication coding system responds to behavioral targets and reveal insufficient sensitivity in some dimensions. Conclusions. SIMAX generates controlled and reproducible simulated clinician-patient dialogues, providing a data foundation for developing, validating, and refining communication coding systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SIMAX：一种可扩展且可解释的多保真度和标注临床医患对话模拟框架
背景：环境式数字记录员的广泛应用正推动大规模采集医患对话数据。人工编码临床沟通数据仍成本高昂、不一致且难以扩展，因此亟需AI驱动的沟通编码系统。然而，评估这些系统需要真实对话和人工标注标签，两者在大规模获取上均较困难。 方法：我们开发了SIMAX（多保真度和标注临床医患对话模拟的可扩展且可解释框架），用于生成带参考行为标注的受控临床对话数据。SIMAX根据预定义的临床场景、人物设定和语音条件，生成医患对话，并针对目标沟通行为进行控制。行为控制通过两个代码本实现：全局代码本用于整体沟通质量，WISER代码本用于可计数的具体行为。我们通过自动化和人工质量评估，以及一个示例沟通编码系统对SIMAX进行评估。 结果：SIMAX生成了涵盖三个专科、多个就诊阶段、人物特征和口音条件的3,388个模拟对话。自动化评估显示平均UTMOS和WV-MOS得分为3.03和2.61，WER和CER分别为0.07和0.05，CLAP余弦相似度为0.41，表明语音自然度合理、转录准确度高、文本与音频对应良好。人工评估显示中位MOS为4.67，中位临床真实度评分为3.00。下游评估表明，SIMAX可评估沟通编码系统对行为目标的响应，并揭示某些维度上敏感度不足。 结论：SIMAX生成受控且可重复的模拟医患对话，为开发、验证和优化沟通编码系统提供数据基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, consistent clinical dialogue data to train and evaluate AI-driven communication coding systems, the authors developed SIMAX, a framework that generates annotated clinician-patient dialogues using predefined clinical scenarios, personas, and voice conditions. SIMAX controls communication behaviors via two codebooks: a Global Codebook for overall quality and a WISER Codebook for specific behaviors. Experimental results show SIMAX produced 3,388 simulated dialogues across multiple specialties and conditions, achieving high transcription accuracy (WER 0.07, CER 0.05) and reasonable speech naturalness (UTMOS 3.03, WV-MOS 2.61). Human evaluators rated the dialogues with a median MOS of 4.67 and clinical realism of 3.00, and downstream tests revealed the framework’s utility in probing communication coding systems’ sensitivity to behavioral targets.
为解决人工标注临床对话数据成本高、难以规模化的问题，作者构建了SIMAX框架，通过预设临床场景、人物设定和语音条件生成带行为标注的模拟医患对话。该框架采用全局代码本与WISER代码本分别控制整体沟通质量与具体行为。实验结果显示，SIMAX生成了3388个覆盖多专科、多就诊阶段和口音条件的模拟对话，语音自然度（UTMOS 3.03）、转录准确率（WER 0.07）和文本-语音一致性（CLAP余弦值0.41）表现良好；人工评估认为对话自然度中位数为4.67，临床真实度中位数为3.00；下游测试表明该框架能有效检验沟通编码系统对行为目标的敏感性，揭示部分维度存在不足。
Factorizable Normalizing Flows for parameter-dependent density morphing
Authors: Davide Valsecchi, Mauro Donegà, Rainer Wallny
First: 2026-06-29T15:54:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T15:54:35+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 8 figures. Code: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21011625
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Normalizing Flows excel at modeling a single fixed density, yet many problems across the sciences, such as high energy physics, instead require modeling how that density deforms as a function of continuous parameters: the strength of a physical effect, a calibration constant, or a source of systematic uncertainty. Learning a separate flow for every parameter configuration quickly becomes intractable, since the number of joint settings grows exponentially with the number of parameters. We introduce Factorizable Normalizing Flows (FNFs), which represent the parameter-dependent density as a fixed, high-fidelity flow for a reference configuration composed with a learnable transformation that is polynomial in the parameters and factorized over them. This structure has a practical consequence: each parameter's effect is learned in isolation, from samples in which that parameter alone is varied. The combined response of many parameters is then recovered by summation at inference, without ever sampling their combinatorially large joint space. On a controlled problem with two interpretable deformations applied jointly to the data, the learned transformation reproduces the true deformations and matches the optimal likelihood, while optional interaction terms capture residual correlations when several parameters vary strongly at once. The resulting model is interpretable, scales linearly with the number of parameters, and keeps the likelihood tractable. This provides a general tool for any inference workflow requiring continuous density morphing, and directly enables the next generation of unbinned likelihood fits in high energy physics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于参数依赖密度变形的可分解归一化流
归一化流擅长建模单一固定密度，但许多科学问题（如高能物理）需要建模该密度随连续参数的变化：物理效应强度、校准常数或系统不确定性来源。为每个参数配置学习独立的流很快变得不可行，因为联合设置的数量随参数数量呈指数增长。我们引入可分解归一化流（FNFs），将参数依赖密度表示为参考配置的固定高保真流，与一个可学习的、参数多项式且按参数分解的变换组合。该结构带来实际优势：每个参数的影响独立学习，仅从该参数单独变化的样本中获取；多个参数的综合响应在推理时通过求和恢复，无需采样其组合爆炸性的联合空间。在两个可解释变形联合作用于数据的受控问题中，学习到的变换重现真实变形并匹配最优似然，可选的交互项捕捉多个参数同时强变化时的残余相关性。所得模型可解释、随参数数量线性扩展、且保持似然可计算。这为任何需要连续密度变形的推断工作流提供通用工具，并直接支持高能物理中下一代无分箱似然拟合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model how probability densities deform continuously with parameters in scientific domains like high energy physics, the paper proposes Factorizable Normalizing Flows (FNFs) to avoid the exponential scaling of learning separate flows for every parameter combination. FNFs represent the parameter-dependent density as a fixed reference flow combined with a learnable, polynomial, and factorized transformation over parameters, enabling each parameter’s effect to be learned independently from single-parameter samples. At inference, the combined effect is obtained by summing parameter-specific transformations without sampling the combinatorially large joint space. Experiments on a controlled two-parameter problem show that FNFs accurately reproduce true deformations, match optimal likelihood, and capture residual correlations via optional interaction terms, while maintaining linear scaling with parameter count and tractable likelihood.
为应对科学领域（如高能物理）中概率密度随连续参数变化的建模需求，本文提出可分解归一化流（FNFs），通过将密度分解为固定参考流与可学习的、多项式且参数可分解的变换，实现高效参数依赖密度形变建模。每个参数的影响独立从单参数样本中学习，推理时通过求和获得联合效应，无需采样组合爆炸的联合空间。在控制的双参数实验中，FNFs准确复现真实形变、匹配最优似然，并通过可选交互项捕捉强参数变化下的残余相关性，同时保持参数数量线性扩展与似然可计算性。
Situation Perception: A Necessary Primitive to Artificial Superintelligence
Authors: Ziqin Yuan, Jaymari Chua
First: 2026-06-29T15:42:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T15:42:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current large language models are extraordinary statistical engines. They compress vast amounts of text into useful patterns and can explain science, write code, imitate reasoning, and participate in philosophical conversation. Yet pattern mastery is not the same as general intelligence. A human infant begins with little explicit knowledge, but gradually discovers object permanence, cause and effect, other minds, bodily agency, and the persistence of the physical world. We make an argument that the path to artificial superintelligence (ASI) depends on a missing capacity we call \emph{situation perception}: the ability to construct, revise, and act within internal simulations of possible worlds across latent time. \emph{ perception} requires at least three core components: abstract prediction, long-term compressed memory, and active learning guided by objectives. In this work, we analyse why modern large language models remain incomplete, and propose the appropriate tests for measuring progress and consequences of machines that can simulate futures, pursue self-directed goals, and possibly judge their own creators.
中文标题/摘要
标题：情境感知：人工超级智能不可或缺的原始能力
当前大型语言模型是卓越的统计引擎，能将大量文本压缩为有用模式，可解释科学、编写代码、模仿推理并参与哲学对话。然而，模式掌握并不等同于通用智能。人类婴儿初始知识有限，但逐渐发现客体恒常性、因果关系、他者心智、身体自主性及物理世界的持续性。我们认为，通往人工超级智能（ASI）的道路依赖于一种缺失的能力——我们称之为\emph{情境感知}：即在潜在时间维度上构建、修订并行动于可能世界内部模拟的能力。\emph{感知}至少需要三个核心要素：抽象预测、长期压缩记忆及由目标引导的主动学习。本文分析现代大型语言模型为何仍不完整，并提出衡量能模拟未来、追求自主目标、甚至可能评判自身创造者的机器进步与后果的适当测试。
Summary / 总结
The paper argues that current large language models, despite their impressive pattern-recognition abilities, lack a fundamental capacity for situation perception — the ability to simulate possible futures, maintain long-term contextual memory, and act purposefully within those simulations. This capacity, essential for artificial superintelligence, is absent in current models because they lack abstract prediction, compressed memory over latent time, and objective-driven learning. The authors propose that true progress toward ASI requires machines capable of constructing internal simulations of possible worlds, pursuing self-directed goals, and evaluating their creators — a framework they suggest as a necessary test for evaluating such systems.
本文指出，当前大型语言模型虽能出色处理文本模式，但缺乏‘情境感知’这一关键能力——即在潜时间维度内构建、修改并行动于可能世界的内部模拟，这是实现人工超智能的必要基础。作者认为，真正的通用智能需具备抽象预测、长期压缩记忆与目标驱动学习三大核心要素，而现有模型尚未具备这些能力。研究分析表明，若不发展此能力，模型将仅停留在统计引擎层面，无法实现真正的自主行动或自我判断，同时提出了衡量机器模拟未来与自主目标能力进展的必要测试标准。
SPARKLING: Balancing Signal Preservation and Symmetry Breaking for Width-Progressive Learning
Authors: Qifan Yu, Xinyu Ma, Zhijian Zhuo, Minrui Wang, Deyi Liu, Shiyi Zhan, Yiyuan Ma, Liang Xiang, Xingyan Bin, Di He
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-02T18:52:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T15:41:30+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 camera-ready version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Progressive Learning (PL) reduces pre-training computational overhead by gradually increasing model scale. While prior work has extensively explored depth expansion, width expansion remains significantly understudied, with the few existing methods limited to the early stages of training. However, expanding width during the mid-stage is essential for maximizing computational savings, yet it remains a formidable challenge due to severe training instabilities. Empirically, we show that naive initialization at this stage disrupts activation statistics, triggering loss spikes, while copy-based initialization introduces gradient symmetry that hinders feature diversity. To address these issues, we propose SPARKLING (balancing {S}ignal {P}reservation {A}nd symmet{R}y brea{K}ing for width-progressive {L}earn{ING}), a novel framework for mid-stage width expansion. Our method achieves signal preservation via RMS-scale consistency, stabilizing activation statistics during expansion. Symmetry breaking is ensured through asymmetric optimizer state reset and asymmetric learning rate re-warmup. Extensive experiments on dense and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models demonstrate that, across multiple width axes and optimizer families, SPARKLING consistently outperforms training from scratch and reduces training cost by up to 35% under $2\times$ width expansion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SPARKLING：在宽度渐进学习中平衡信号保留与对称性打破
渐进学习（PL）通过逐步增加模型规模来降低预训练计算开销。虽然已有大量研究探索深度扩展，但宽度扩展仍被严重忽视，现有方法仅限于训练早期阶段。然而，在中期扩展宽度对于最大化计算节省至关重要，但因训练不稳定而极具挑战。实证表明，该阶段的朴素初始化会破坏激活统计，引发损失激增；而基于复制的初始化则引入梯度对称，阻碍特征多样性。为解决这些问题，我们提出SPARKLING（平衡信号保留与对称性打破的宽度渐进学习框架），通过RMS尺度一致性实现信号保留，稳定扩展过程中的激活统计；通过非对称优化器状态重置与非对称学习率重新预热确保对称性打破。在稠密模型与专家混合（MoE）模型上的大量实验表明，SPARKLING在多个宽度维度和优化器家族中均优于从头训练，并在2倍宽度扩展下将训练成本降低最多35%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce pre-training costs in progressive learning, this paper addresses the challenge of mid-stage width expansion, which is critical for maximizing computational savings but suffers from training instability. The authors propose SPARKLING, a framework that balances signal preservation and symmetry breaking: it stabilizes activations via RMS-scale consistency and breaks gradient symmetry through asymmetric optimizer state reset and learning rate re-warmup. Experiments on dense and MoE models show SPARKLING consistently outperforms training from scratch and reduces training cost by up to 35% under double width expansion.
为降低预训练计算开销，SPARKLING提出在渐进式学习中期进行宽度扩展的框架，兼顾信号保持与对称性打破。通过RMS尺度一致性稳定激活统计，结合非对称优化器状态重置与学习率重暖启动打破梯度对称性。在稠密与MoE模型上实验表明，SPARKLING持续优于从头训练，在双倍宽度扩展下可降低35%训练成本。
COHORT: Collaborative Orchestration for Hardening via Offensive Replay on Emulated Topologies
Authors: Chen Frydman, Aviram Zilberman, Rubin Krief, Abed Showgan, Andres Murillo, Sekiya Motoyoshi, Asaf Shabtai, Yuval Elovici, Rami Puzis
First: 2026-06-29T15:39:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T15:39:05+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Journal of Network and Computer Applications
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mitigating an observed adversary in an enterprise network typically takes weeks of expert work: an analyst derives a mitigation tailored to that adversary, validates it without breaking production, and verifies it disrupts the specific attack. The procedure relies on expert judgment and cannot safely be exercised against the production network. COHORT is the first end-to-end framework to automate this procedure for deployable mitigations. A role-decomposed multi-agent LLM workflow proposes candidates, implements them as real device commands, and refines them through a critique loop, all on a high-fidelity GNS3 emulator running real vendor firmware (firewall, switch, router). Each candidate is evaluated by offensive replay: re-executing the original adversary on the mitigated network for a paired comparison against the unmitigated baseline, rather than the reward-signal or expert-judgment proxies used in prior simulation, hybrid, and configuration-generation work. Two further checks complement replay: a connectivity-regression check (LAN ping and internet HTTP probe) rejects mitigations that disrupt legitimate LAN or internet connectivity, and a cumulative evaluation stacks approved mitigations onto a persistent state to surface compound effects. Across three topologies and four attack scenarios (ransomware, lateral movement, DNS exfiltration, data theft), 46.7% of generated mitigations both disrupt the attack and preserve connectivity under replay, 4.4 times the rate of a single-agent baseline using the same model and tool access. A demo video walking through the framework is available with our released artifacts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：COHORT：通过模拟拓扑上的进攻重放实现协作编排的网络加固框架
在企业网络中应对观察到的攻击者通常需要数周专家工作：分析师需定制化制定缓解措施，验证其不破坏生产环境，并确认其能阻断特定攻击。该流程依赖专家判断，无法安全应用于生产网络。COHORT是首个实现可部署缓解措施端到端自动化的框架。其采用角色分解的多智能体大语言模型工作流，提出候选方案、将其转化为真实设备命令，并通过批判循环进行优化，所有操作均在高保真GNS3模拟器上执行（运行真实厂商固件：防火墙、交换机、路由器）。每个候选方案通过进攻重放评估：在已实施缓解措施的网络上重新执行原始攻击者，与未缓解的基线进行配对比较，而非此前仿真、混合或配置生成工作中使用的奖励信号或专家判断代理。此外，两个补充检查机制：连通性回归检查（局域网ping和互联网HTTP探测）拒绝破坏合法局域网或互联网连通性的缓解措施；累积评估将已批准的缓解措施叠加到持久化状态，以揭示复合效应。在三个拓扑结构和四种攻击场景（勒索软件、横向移动、DNS数据外泄、数据窃取）中，46.7%的生成缓解措施在重放中既阻断攻击又保持连通性，是使用相同模型和工具访问的单智能体基线的4.4倍。随我们发布的成果附有演示视频，可浏览框架操作流程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and risk of manually crafting network mitigations against observed adversaries, COHORT automates the entire mitigation workflow using a role-decomposed multi-agent LLM system that generates, implements, and refines defensive configurations on a GNS3 emulator running real vendor firmware. The framework evaluates each mitigation through offensive replay—re-running the original attack against the modified network to directly compare its effectiveness against an unmitigated baseline—while also enforcing connectivity checks and cumulative evaluation to avoid breaking legitimate traffic or masking compound effects. Across three network topologies and four attack types, COHORT produced mitigations that successfully disrupted attacks and preserved connectivity in 46.7% of cases, a rate 4.4 times higher than a single-agent baseline using identical tools and models.
为解决人工制定网络防御措施效率低、风险高的问题，COHORT 采用多智能体LLM系统，在运行真实厂商固件的高保真GNS3仿真环境中，自动化生成、部署并优化防御配置。其评估机制通过“攻击重演”——即在防御后重新执行原始攻击并对比未防御基线——结合连通性回归检查与累积效应分析，确保防御有效性与网络正常运行。在三个网络拓扑和四种攻击场景下，该框架成功率达46.7%，显著优于单一智能体基线（提升4.4倍）。
PGE-SAM: Prompt-Guided Feature Enhancement for Interactive Segmentation under Degradation
Authors: Tuan-Duc Nguyen, Anh-Tuan Mai, Duc-Trong Le
First: 2026-06-29T15:37:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T15:37:47+00:00
Comments: 54 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Segment Anything Model (SAM) has revolutionized promptable image segmentation with strong zero-shot generalization. However, its performance degrades substantially under real-world imaging artifacts such as noise, blur, and compression. Existing methods restore features globally without focusing on segmentation-relevant regions and neglect SAM's iterative refinement mechanism, leading to suboptimal performance in interactive settings. We propose Prompt-Guided Feature Enhancement SAM (PGE-SAM), a framework that explicitly leverages user prompts and prior mask predictions to spatially guide the feature restoration process toward regions of interest through a Prompt Guidance Generator. To recover fine-grained details lost under degradation, we introduce Multi-Scale Features Interaction to incorporate low-level encoder features, along with a Foreground Reconstruction Loss that restricts feature-level supervision to the segmentation target. Furthermore, we present DM-Seg, a benchmark for interactive segmentation on degraded medical images, spanning multiple imaging modalities with both general and modality-specific degradations at varying severity levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PGE-SAM achieves SOTA robustness on both medical and natural image domains across multiple degradation levels, while maintaining generalization to clean images and adding less than one-fifth of the parameters of prior methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PGE-SAM：面向退化图像的提示引导特征增强交互分割方法
分割任意模型（SAM）凭借强大的零样本泛化能力，革新了可提示图像分割。然而，在现实世界成像伪影（如噪声、模糊和压缩）下，其性能显著下降。现有方法全局恢复特征，未聚焦分割相关区域，且忽略SAM的迭代优化机制，导致交互场景下性能不佳。本文提出提示引导特征增强SAM（PGE-SAM），通过提示引导生成器，显式利用用户提示与先验掩码预测，空间引导特征恢复聚焦于感兴趣区域。为恢复退化导致的细粒度细节，引入多尺度特征交互，融合低层编码器特征，并设计前景重建损失，将特征级监督限制于分割目标。此外，我们提出DM-Seg，一个面向退化医学图像的交互分割基准，涵盖多种成像模态及不同程度的一般性与模态特异性退化。大量实验表明，PGE-SAM在多个退化级别下，于医学与自然图像领域均达到最先进鲁棒性，同时保持对干净图像的泛化能力，且参数量仅为先前方法的五分之一以下。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by SAM's strong zero-shot segmentation but poor performance under real-world image degradations like noise and blur, PGE-SAM introduces a prompt-guided feature restoration mechanism that spatially focuses enhancement on segmentation-relevant regions using user prompts and prior mask predictions. It employs a Prompt Guidance Generator to direct feature recovery and integrates Multi-Scale Features Interaction to recover fine details, while a Foreground Reconstruction Loss ensures supervision targets only the segmentation foreground. Evaluated on the newly proposed DM-Seg benchmark for degraded medical images, PGE-SAM achieves state-of-the-art robustness across multiple degradation levels and image domains, while maintaining clean-image generalization and using fewer than one-fifth the parameters of prior methods.
为解决SAM在噪声、模糊等真实图像退化下性能显著下降的问题，本文提出PGE-SAM框架，通过提示引导生成器利用用户提示和前序掩码预测，空间引导特征恢复聚焦于感兴趣区域。结合多尺度特征交互与前景重建损失，强化对分割目标的特征监督，恢复细粒度细节。在新提出的DM-Seg医学图像退化基准上，PGE-SAM在多种退化程度下均达到最优鲁棒性，同时保持对干净图像的良好泛化能力，参数量仅为先前方法的五分之一以下。
SOTAlign: Semi-Supervised Alignment of Unimodal Vision and Language Models via Optimal Transport
Authors: Simon Roschmann, Paul Krzakala, Sonia Mazelet, Quentin Bouniot, Zeynep Akata
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-26T18:55:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T15:37:39+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The Platonic Representation Hypothesis posits that neural networks trained on different modalities converge toward a shared statistical model of the world. Recent work exploits this convergence by aligning frozen pretrained vision and language models with lightweight alignment layers, but typically relies on contrastive losses and millions of paired samples. In this work, we ask whether meaningful alignment can be achieved with substantially less supervision. We introduce a semi-supervised setting in which pretrained unimodal encoders are aligned using a small number of image-text pairs together with large amounts of unpaired data. To address this challenge, we propose SOTAlign, a two-stage framework that first recovers a coarse shared geometry from limited paired data using a linear teacher, and then refines the alignment on unpaired samples via an optimal-transport-based divergence that transfers relational structure without overconstraining the target space. SOTAlign effectively leverages unpaired images and text, learning robust joint embeddings across datasets and encoder pairs, and significantly outperforming supervised and semi-supervised baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/SOTAlign.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SOTAlign：通过最优传输实现单模态视觉与语言模型的半监督对齐
柏拉图表征假设认为，不同模态训练的神经网络会收敛到对世界的共同统计模型。近期工作通过在冻结的预训练视觉与语言模型上添加轻量级对齐层来利用这一收敛性，但通常依赖对比损失和数百万对样本。本文探讨是否能在显著减少监督的情况下实现有意义的对齐。我们引入一种半监督设置，在少量图像-文本对与大量未配对数据的基础上对预训练单模态编码器进行对齐。为此，我们提出SOTAlign，一种两阶段框架：首先利用线性教师从有限配对数据中恢复粗略的共享几何结构；然后通过基于最优传输的散度，在未配对样本上细化对齐，从而在不过度约束目标空间的前提下传递关系结构。SOTAlign有效利用未配对图像与文本，学习跨数据集和编码器对的鲁棒联合嵌入，并显著优于监督与半监督基线方法。代码见https://github.com/ExplainableML/SOTAlign。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the Platonic Representation Hypothesis and the desire to reduce reliance on large paired datasets, this work proposes SOTAlign, a semi-supervised method that aligns frozen unimodal vision and language encoders using only a small number of image-text pairs and abundant unpaired data. The method employs a two-stage approach: first, a linear teacher model learns a coarse shared geometry from the limited paired samples; then, optimal transport-based divergence is used to refine alignment on unpaired data, preserving relational structure without overconstraining the target space. Experiments show SOTAlign achieves robust joint embeddings across diverse datasets and encoder pairs, significantly outperforming both supervised and other semi-supervised baselines.
受柏拉图表征假设启发，为减少对大量配对数据的依赖，本文提出SOTAlign方法，通过少量图像-文本对与大量未配对数据实现冻结的单模态视觉与语言模型对齐。该方法采用两阶段策略：首先，线性教师模型从有限配对数据中学习粗略共享几何结构；随后，基于最优传输的散度在未配对数据上精细对齐，通过传递关系结构而不过度约束目标空间。实验表明，SOTAlign在多个数据集和编码器组合上学习到鲁棒的联合嵌入，显著优于监督和其它半监督基线方法。
